=== Test Procedures

Unless a test procedure states otherwise, each test starts from the common DTP
bring-up sequence: build `dtp_uvm_top`, start `clk_i`, assert and release
`pwr_on_rst_ni`/`rst_n_i`, reset the primary TAP, use the configured OCAH
BFMs, and keep all `dbg_disable_i` fields cleared (full debug access). Each
procedure ends by checking scoreboard/reference-model errors are zero.

[[sec-0-smoke-and-basic-jtag]]
==== 0. Smoke and Basic JTAG

[[sec-01-dtp_sanity_test]]
===== 0.1 `dtp_sanity_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: TAP FSM coverage: reset, DR/IR/pause path traversal, randomized state navigation, all IEEE 1149.1 states visited

*Test Coverage*:

* Core TMS/TCK/TDI/TDO datapath driven through a complete IR/DR scan sequence
* TAP FSM traversal of all targeted IEEE 1149.1 states: TLR, RTI, Select-DR/IR, Capture, Shift, Pause, Update
* A fixed TMS walk from Test-Logic-Reset takes all 32 legal state/TMS transitions, and seeded random navigation with random TDI adds paths on top
* BYPASS load (IR=6 bits, 0x00) produces a single-cycle TDI-to-TDO latency during Shift-DR
* Test-Logic-Reset selects IDCODE: a DR scan with no IR load reads it, and IDCODE reads through explicit IR loads are stable
* `cg_tap_state` and `cg_tap_arc` bins, the `cg_tap_smoke` TMS reset, IDCODE read, and marker bins, and the `cg_jtag_instruction` Capture-IR pattern bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pulse `pwr_on_rst_ni` and `rst_n_i`, set every `dbg_disable_i` field to 1, and reset the primary TAP to Test-Logic-Reset. The disables stay set for the pass, since the random walks shift random TDI into whatever instruction they load.
. Walk a fixed TMS sequence from Test-Logic-Reset that takes all 32 legal transitions and returns to Test-Logic-Reset.
. Through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`, sequence TLR → RTI → Select-DR → Capture-DR → Shift-DR → Update-DR → RTI, sampling `jtag_ptap_state_o` after each TCK.
. Drive an IR scan RTI → Select-DR → Select-IR → Capture-IR → Shift-IR (6 bits, BYPASS 0x00) → Update-IR → RTI.
. With BYPASS active, run directed and seeded 64-bit DR scans and confirm the single-TCK TDI-to-TDO delay.
. Force TLR with five TMS=1 cycles and step to Run-Test/Idle; a 32-bit DR scan with no IR load reads IDCODE. Read IDCODE again through explicit IR loads with a BYPASS load between the reads.
. Drive randomized raw-TMS paths with random TDI through DR/IR/pause/update legs.
. Return to TLR via at least five TMS=1 cycles.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sanity_fsm_visit_chk` |Every targeted IEEE 1149.1 state and legal transition is observed on `jtag_ptap_state_o`: each raw TMS step against the TAP reference model (`CHK-TAP-STATE`), each navigation landing on its target (`CHK-TAP-GOTO`), and all sixteen states and all 32 legal transitions within the pass (`CHK-TAP-VISIT-ALL`)
|`sanity_bypass_latency_chk` |BYPASS (IR=0x00) decodes as itself and yields exactly a 1-TCK TDI-to-TDO delay in Shift-DR (`CHK-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-BYPASS-LATENCY`); at least three patterns scan through it, and at least one returns a delayed image that differs from the pattern (`CHK-NONVAC`)
|`sanity_idcode_chk` |A DR scan right after Test-Logic-Reset, with no IR load, reads IDCODE (`CHK-TAP-TLR-IDCODE`), and IDCODE reads across IR churn equal the default value, match each other, and carry the marker bit (`CHK-IDCODE-RAW`, `CHK-IDCODE-STABLE`, `CHK-IDCODE-MARKER`)
|`sanity_scan_path_chk` |IR/DR scan sequence completes, the TAP returns to Run-Test/Idle cleanly, and five TMS-high cycles force Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-TAP-RESET-TLR`, `CHK-TAP-TLR-TMS5`); each IR and DR scan is one new Shift-IR or Shift-DR visit of the DUT's exported TAP state, as long as the width driven (`CHK-SCAN-IR-LEN`, `CHK-SCAN-DR-LEN`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The TAP sequences correctly through all targeted states with every IEEE 1149.1 state visited and every legal transition taken, BYPASS showing 1-bit latency, and Test-Logic-Reset selecting IDCODE, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-02-dtp_jtag_bypass_test]]
===== 0.2 `dtp_jtag_bypass_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: BYPASS `0x00` and `0x3F`

*Test Coverage*:

* Both BYPASS encodings (IR=0x00 and IR=0x3F) present a 1-bit DR shift register between TDI and TDO
* TDO equals TDI delayed by exactly one TCK cycle during Shift-DR for each encoding
* Capture-DR loads 0 into the bypass register per IEEE 1149.1
* `cg_jtag_instruction` opcode bins 0x00 and 0x3F and the `cg_dr_scan` `bypass_alt` and `bypass` bins

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load IR = 0x00 (BYPASS) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Shift a known 64-bit pattern (alternating 0/1) through DR and confirm TDO == TDI delayed by exactly 1 bit.
. Verify Capture-DR loaded 0 into the bypass register (leading captured bit is 0).
. Load IR = 0x3F (BYPASS all-ones) and repeat the same 64-bit pattern shift.
. Confirm identical 1-bit-delay behavior and DR length of 1 for both encodings.
. Return to Run-Test/Idle.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`bypass_dr_len_chk` |DR chain length is exactly 1 bit for both IR=0x00 and IR=0x3F
|`bypass_latency_chk` |TDO equals TDI delayed by one TCK cycle during Shift-DR
|`bypass_capture_chk` |Capture-DR loads 0 into the bypass register
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Both BYPASS encodings produce a 1-bit register with one-cycle TDI-to-TDO delay and a 0 capture value, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-03-dtp_jtag_undef_instr_test]]
===== 0.3 `dtp_jtag_undef_instr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Undefined instructions select BYPASS

*Test Coverage*:

* All undefined/reserved IR encodings (0x0F, 0x10-0x17, 0x2D-0x3C) decode as themselves and default to BYPASS
* Each reserved opcode produces 1-bit bypass behavior (TDI -++>++ 1-bit delay -++>++ TDO)
* No instruction-qualified host chain select (boundary scan, secure DFT, non-secure DFT, DFD) rises across a reserved-opcode DR scan, and the debug-TDR pin outputs keep their reset image; the extended STAP host interface stays quiet while the PTAP 3DCR SELECT is clear and is judged by the STAP scenarios
* `cg_jtag_instruction` reserved and undefined opcode bins and the `cg_dr_scan` `reserved` and `undefined` bins

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each reserved encoding:
a. Load the reserved IR value through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and confirm the decoded-instruction one-hot names it.
b. Run a 64-bit DR scan with a known pattern under a scan-control window over the four instruction-qualified host selects: TDO is TDI delayed one TCK and every select counts zero pulses.
c. Run a seeded random 32-bit DR scan and confirm the same 1-bit bypass behavior.
. After the sweep, sample the debug-TDR pin outputs (clock-stop, boot-stall, IC_RESET slices) and confirm the reset image.
. On a seeded sample of the reserved encodings, run the full directed-pattern bypass sweep.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`undef_bypass_fallback_chk` |Each reserved IR opcode decodes as itself (`CHK-IR-DECODE`) and selects the 1-bit BYPASS path (`CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`undef_no_tdr_select_chk` |None of the four instruction-qualified host chain selects rises across a reserved-opcode DR scan, and the debug-TDR pin outputs keep their reset image afterwards (`CHK-UNDEF-NO-SELECT`)
|`undef_no_error_chk` |The pass finalizes with zero failed or missing family checks and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors (`CHECKER_SUMMARY`, the `CHK-SB-*` records)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every tested reserved/undefined IR encoding decodes as itself and falls back to 1-bit BYPASS with no host chain selected and the debug-TDR outputs unchanged, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-04-dtp_jtag_idcode_test]]
===== 0.4 `dtp_jtag_idcode_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: IDCODE field verification

*Test Coverage*:

* 32-bit IDCODE TDR auto-loaded on power-up and after Test-Logic-Reset
* Field decode: `SI_REV[31:28]`, `PART_NUM[27:12]`, `MFR_ID[11:1]`, `MARKER[0]=1`
* IDCODE equals `++{++JTAG_IDCODE_SI_REV, JTAG_IDCODE_PART_NUM, JTAG_IDCODE_MFR_ID, 1'b1}`
* IDCODE consistency across power-up, TLR, and explicit IDCODE instruction load
* `cg_tap_smoke` IDCODE bins: reads after TRST and after power-on reset with no IR load, a read after an explicit IR load, and the marker bit

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the primary TAP to Test-Logic-Reset so IDCODE auto-loads.
. Without loading any IR, perform a 32-bit DR scan through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and capture TDO.
. Decode and check fields `SI_REV[31:28]`, `PART_NUM[27:12]`, `MFR_ID[11:1]`, and `MARKER[0] == 1`.
. Load a seeded instruction other than IDCODE, hold `pwr_on_rst_ni` low for 2..8 TCK periods with TRST_N high and TCK idle, step Test-Logic-Reset to Run-Test/Idle with TMS low, and perform a 32-bit DR scan with no IR load: it reads IDCODE.
. Explicitly load IDCODE (IR=0x01); repeat the 32-bit DR scan.
. Force TLR again and re-read; require an identical value on every read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`idcode_value_chk` |Captured IDCODE equals `++{++SI_REV, PART_NUM, MFR_ID, 1'b1}` from compile-time parameters
|`idcode_marker_chk` |MARKER bit (bit 0) is always 1
|`idcode_power_on_chk` |After a power-on reset with TRST_N high, a DR scan with no IR load reads IDCODE, and the tb_top assertion counter advanced across the pulse (`CHK-IDCODE-RECOVERY`, `CHK-RESET-COUNT`)
|`idcode_stable_chk` |IDCODE identical across power-up, explicit IR load, and post-TLR reads
|===

*Pass Criteria*: IDCODE matches the parameter-defined value with the marker bit set and is stable across all three read paths, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-05-dtp_jtag_runbist_test]]
===== 0.5 `dtp_jtag_runbist_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG/DFT model

*Objective*: RUNBIST and DFT gating

*Test Coverage*:

* RUNBIST (IR=0x02) decodes as itself and selects the iJTAG network as its data register: with every SIB closed that register is the three-SIB chain, so TDO trails TDI by three TCK with the SIB captures in the low bits
* The non-secure DFT host's `runbist` scan-control field follows the RUNBIST instruction decode, whatever the DFT SIB state and the `dft_nonsecure` disable
* `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure` does NOT suppress the RUNBIST instruction itself; it holds the non-secure DFT SIB closed and silences that host's scan controls
* After clearing `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`, the stored SIB open state is effective again and a RUNBIST scan drives the DFT host scan controls without reprogramming the SIB
* `cg_jtag_instruction` RUNBIST opcode bin, the `cg_dr_scan` `runbist` bin, the `cg_instruction_gate` RUNBIST bins with `dft_nonsecure` clear and set, and the `cg_ijtag_sib` `cp_instr` `runbist` bin with its `cp_dft_nonsecure` `open` and `gated` bins

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load RUNBIST (IR=0x02) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; confirm the decoded-instruction one-hot and the DFT host `runbist` strobe. Load BYPASS and confirm the strobe drops; reload RUNBIST.
. Run directed and seeded 8-bit DR scans; compare each with the SIB-chain prediction (the SIB captures, then the pattern three TCK behind TDI) and confirm the responses are distinct and nonzero.
. Open the non-secure DFT SIB through SELECT_IJTAG and reload RUNBIST. Under a scan-control window a RUNBIST DR scan shows the DFT host select high with one capture, eight shift, and one update strobe.
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure = 1`; reload RUNBIST and confirm the decode and the `runbist` strobe are unchanged; the same window over a RUNBIST DR scan that shifts a 0 into the DFT SIB bit shows every DFT host scan control at zero.
. Clear `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`; the window shows the DFT host select and strobes again without reprogramming the SIB.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`runbist_accept_chk` |RUNBIST decodes as itself (`CHK-IR-DECODE`), raises the DFT host `runbist` strobe (`CHK-DFT-RUNBIST`), and its DR scan is the SIB chain: TDO three TCK behind TDI with the SIB captures in the low bits (`CHK-RUNBIST-DR-LEN`), distinct and nonzero across patterns (`CHK-RUNBIST-RESPONSE`)
|`runbist_instr_ungated_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure` asserted the RUNBIST decode and the DFT host `runbist` strobe are unchanged (`CHK-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-DFT-RUNBIST`)
|`dft_sib_gated_chk` |With the disable asserted the DFT host select and its capture/shift/update strobes count zero across a RUNBIST DR scan (`CHK-DFT-SIB-SELECT`, `CHK-DFT-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`dft_sib_restored_chk` |With the disable cleared the stored SIB open state, which the gated scan's 0 did not overwrite, is effective again: select high with one capture, N shift, and one update strobe across the scan (`CHK-DFT-SIB-SELECT`, `CHK-DFT-SCAN-CTRL`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: RUNBIST is always accepted with the SIB chain as its data register and the DFT host `runbist` strobe following its decode, and the non-secure DFT host scan controls follow a RUNBIST scan only while `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure` is clear, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-06-dtp_jtag_sample_preload_test]]
===== 0.6 `dtp_jtag_sample_preload_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: SAMPLE/PRELOAD

*Test Coverage*:

* SAMPLE/PRELOAD (IR=0x03) selects the boundary-scan chain; the bench has no boundary cells and loops the chain back, so a DR scan returns the pattern one TCK late
* The chain's select is high across a SAMPLE/PRELOAD DR scan while the TAP's capture, shift, and update strobes pulse once, N times, and once
* Under BYPASS the chain's select stays low while the same strobes pulse: only select is qualified by the instruction
* `cg_jtag_instruction` SAMPLE/PRELOAD opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `sample_preload` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SAMPLE/PRELOAD (IR=0x03) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and shift directed and seeded patterns; each returns retimed by one TCK through the looped-back chain.
. Open a scan-control window across one N-bit SAMPLE/PRELOAD DR scan: `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` high, capture once, shift N times, update once.
. Load BYPASS and repeat the window across a DR scan: select low, the strobes pulsing, TDO the one-bit bypass path.
. Reload SAMPLE/PRELOAD and confirm the loopback again.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sample_capture_chk` |Capture-DR strobes once and the looped-back chain returns the shifted pattern retimed by one TCK; the bench has no boundary cells, so pin capture itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|`preload_no_drive_chk` |Under BYPASS the boundary-scan select stays low across a DR scan while the TAP's strobes pulse, so no boundary update can occur; the bench has no boundary cells, so pin drive itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`preload_retain_chk` |Across a SAMPLE/PRELOAD DR scan the select is high with one capture, N shift, and one update strobe, the protocol a boundary register latches its preload on (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: SAMPLE/PRELOAD selects the looped-back chain with the capture, shift, and update strobes of an N-bit scan and returns the pattern retimed by one TCK, while BYPASS leaves the select low, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-07-dtp_jtag_extest_test]]
===== 0.7 `dtp_jtag_extest_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: EXTEST

*Test Coverage*:

* EXTEST (IR=0x04) selects the boundary-scan chain; the bench has no boundary cells and loops the chain back, so a DR scan returns the pattern one TCK late
* `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` is high across an EXTEST DR scan while its capture, shift, and update strobes pulse once, N times, and once
* Under BYPASS the select stays low while the same strobes pulse: only select is qualified by the instruction
* `cg_jtag_instruction` EXTEST opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `extest` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SAMPLE/PRELOAD (IR=0x03) and shift a known pattern through the looped-back chain.
. Load EXTEST (IR=0x04) and shift directed and seeded patterns; each returns retimed by one TCK.
. Open a scan-control window across one N-bit EXTEST DR scan: `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` high, capture once, shift N times, update once.
. Load BYPASS and repeat the window across a DR scan: select low, the strobes pulsing, TDO the one-bit bypass path.
. Reload EXTEST and confirm the loopback again.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`extest_output_drive_chk` |Across an EXTEST DR scan the chain select is high and Update-DR strobes once, the protocol a boundary register drives its pins on; the bench has no boundary cells, so pin drive itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`extest_capture_chk` |Capture-DR strobes once and the looped-back chain returns the shifted pattern retimed by one TCK; the bench has no boundary cells, so pin capture itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|`extest_scan_ctrl_chk` |`jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o`: select high across the EXTEST scan with capture once, shift N times, update once; under BYPASS select stays low while the same strobes pulse (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: EXTEST selects the looped-back chain with the capture, shift, and update strobes of an N-bit scan and returns the pattern retimed by one TCK, while BYPASS leaves the select low, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-08-dtp_jtag_intest_test]]
===== 0.8 `dtp_jtag_intest_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: INTEST

*Test Coverage*:

* INTEST (IR=0x09) selects the boundary-scan chain; the bench has no boundary cells and loops the chain back, so a DR scan returns the pattern one TCK late
* `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` is high across an INTEST DR scan while its capture, shift, and update strobes pulse once, N times, and once
* Under BYPASS the select stays low while the same strobes pulse: only select is qualified by the instruction
* `cg_jtag_instruction` INTEST opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `intest` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SAMPLE/PRELOAD (IR=0x03) and shift a known pattern through the looped-back chain.
. Load INTEST (IR=0x09) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and shift directed and seeded patterns; each returns retimed by one TCK.
. Open a scan-control window across one N-bit INTEST DR scan: `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` high, capture once, shift N times, update once.
. Load BYPASS and repeat the window across a DR scan: select low, the strobes pulsing, TDO the one-bit bypass path.
. Reload INTEST and confirm the loopback again.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`intest_input_drive_chk` |Across an INTEST DR scan the chain select is high and Update-DR strobes once, the protocol a boundary register drives the core inputs on; the bench has no boundary cells, so the internal drive itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`intest_capture_chk` |Capture-DR strobes once and the looped-back chain returns the shifted pattern retimed by one TCK; the bench has no boundary cells, so the internal capture itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|`intest_scan_ctrl_chk` |`jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o`: select high across the INTEST scan with capture once, shift N times, update once; under BYPASS select stays low while the same strobes pulse (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: INTEST selects the looped-back chain with the capture, shift, and update strobes of an N-bit scan and returns the pattern retimed by one TCK, while BYPASS leaves the select low, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-09-dtp_jtag_clamp_test]]
===== 0.9 `dtp_jtag_clamp_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: CLAMP

*Test Coverage*:

* CLAMP (IR=0x07) leaves the boundary-scan chain deselected: the chain select stays low across CLAMP scans while the TAP's capture, shift, and update strobes pulse, so the update register that drives the chain's outputs receives no update
* A 1-bit bypass path (TDI -++>++ 1-bit delay -++>++ TDO) is provided on DR during CLAMP
* EXTEST before and after CLAMP selects the looped-back chain and returns its pattern
* `cg_jtag_instruction` CLAMP opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `clamp` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load EXTEST and scan a seeded pattern under a scan-control window: the chain select is high across the scan and the looped-back pattern returns one TCK late.
. Load CLAMP (IR=0x07) and scan under the same window: the select stays low while the capture, shift, and update strobes pulse once each, and TDO is TDI delayed one TCK.
. Shift the directed and seeded one-bit bypass patterns through CLAMP.
. Load EXTEST again and scan another seeded pattern: the chain is selected again and loops the pattern back.
. Load BYPASS and confirm the one-TCK delay as the reference.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`clamp_deselect_chk` |The chain select stays low across the CLAMP scans while the TAP strobes pulse (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`clamp_bypass_chk` |DR path is a 1-bit bypass during CLAMP (`CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`clamp_chain_restore_chk` |EXTEST before and after CLAMP selects the chain and returns the looped-back pattern (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CLAMP scans leave the chain deselected while DR acts as a 1-bit bypass, and EXTEST selects the looped-back chain before and after, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-010-dtp_jtag_highz_test]]
===== 0.10 `dtp_jtag_highz_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: HIGHZ

*Test Coverage*:

* HIGHZ (IR=0x08) decodes as itself and scans the one-bit bypass register
* The boundary-scan select stays low across a HIGHZ DR scan while the TAP's strobes pulse; the bench has no boundary cells and no boundary output enables (its only `+_oen_o+` ports are the TDO enables), so the tristate itself is not observable
* A boundary-scan instruction loaded after HIGHZ re-selects the looped-back chain
* `cg_jtag_instruction` HIGHZ opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `highz` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load EXTEST and confirm the loopback through the chain.
. Load HIGHZ (IR=0x08) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; confirm the decoded-instruction one-hot and the 1-bit bypass behavior across the directed and seeded pattern classes.
. Open a scan-control window across a 64-bit HIGHZ DR scan: `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` low, capture once, shift 64 times, update once, TDO the one-bit bypass path.
. Load EXTEST: the same window shows select high across its scan and the loopback returns.
. Load CLAMP and check its bypass path; load IDCODE; load HIGHZ again and check its bypass path.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`highz_oen_deassert_chk` |HIGHZ decodes as itself and leaves the boundary-scan select low across its DR scan while the TAP's strobes pulse; the bench has no boundary output enables to observe (`CHK-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`highz_bypass_chk` |DR path is a 1-bit bypass during HIGHZ (`CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`highz_recover_chk` |EXTEST loaded after HIGHZ re-selects the looped-back chain: select high across its scan and the loopback returns (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: HIGHZ decodes, scans the 1-bit bypass, and leaves the boundary-scan select low, and a boundary-scan instruction re-selects the chain afterwards, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-011-dtp_jtag_zero_length_bypass_test]]
===== 0.11 `dtp_jtag_zero_length_bypass_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: Zero-length bypass

*Test Coverage*:

* With the PTAP 3DCR select clear, ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS (IR=0x3D) provides a 0-bit DR path (TDI connects directly to TDO with no latency)
* DR chain length is exactly 0 bits, so the TAP effectively disappears from the scan chain
* With the PTAP 3DCR select set and a behavioral downstream TAP spliced behind a seeded I/O, SMC, SEP, or extra STAP, ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS behaves exactly as BYPASS: a DR scan returns the chain captures, then the bypass register's captured 0, then its marker, and matches the same scan under BYPASS
* `cg_jtag_instruction` opcode bin 0x3D (ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS) and the `cg_dr_scan` `zero_length_bypass` bin with both `cp_zero_length_path` bins, the PTAP 3DCR select clear and set

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load IR = 0x3D (ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Attempt a DR scan and confirm the DR chain length measures exactly 0 bits.
. Shift a known pattern and verify TDO reflects TDI with zero additional delay.
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain (TRST, a TAP_3DCR load and an over-length all-zero scan), then with composed TAP_3DCR scans set the PTAP 3DCR select, open the SIB of a seeded I/O, SMC, SEP, or extra STAP, and select it; one network-wide IR scan loads ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS into the PTAP and `DS_TDR` into the downstream TAP.
. Write a per-pass random `DS_TDR` value through the chain with a ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS DR scan, then run a state-maintaining scan: the captured chain matches the reference model, and in both scans the bit after the STAP chain is the bypass register's captured 0 and a seeded marker follows it.
. Load BYPASS (IR=0x3F) with a network-wide IR scan and repeat the maintaining scan: the chain reads back with the written value, and the captured 0 and the marker arrive at the same positions as under ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`zlbypass_len_chk` |DR chain length is exactly 0 bits under IR=0x3D with the PTAP 3DCR select clear
|`zlbypass_passthrough_chk` |TDO reflects TDI with zero added latency with the PTAP 3DCR select clear
|`zlbypass_chain_align_chk` |With the PTAP 3DCR select set, ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS adds the BYPASS bit: through the STAP chain the bypass register's captured 0 follows the chain and the marker follows it, under ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS and BYPASS alike, and the captured chain matches the reference model (`CHK-ZLB-CHAIN-ALIGN`, `CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With the PTAP 3DCR select clear, ZERO_LENGTH_BYPASS yields a 0-bit DR with direct TDI-to-TDO pass-through; with it set, the STAP chain scans exactly as under BYPASS, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-012-dtp_jtag_inv_bypass_test]]
===== 0.12 `dtp_jtag_inv_bypass_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Inverted bypass

*Test Coverage*:

* INV_BYPASS (IR=0x3E) provides a 1-bit bypass register that inverts the shifted data
* TDO is the bitwise inverse of TDI, delayed by exactly one TCK cycle
* Capture-DR loads the inverted default (1) into the bypass register
* IDCODE reads its identification after the inverted-bypass scans
* `cg_jtag_instruction` opcode bin 0x3E (INV_BYPASS) and the `cg_dr_scan` `inv_bypass` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load IR = 0x3E (INV_BYPASS) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Shift a known 64-bit alternating 0/1 pattern through DR and verify TDO = inverted TDI delayed by 1 bit.
. Shift all-zeros and confirm TDO outputs all-ones after the 1-bit delay.
. Shift all-ones and confirm TDO outputs all-zeros after the 1-bit delay.
. Confirm Capture-DR loaded the inverted default (1), then read IDCODE and confirm the identification.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`inv_bypass_len_chk` |DR chain length is exactly 1 bit under IR=0x3E
|`inv_bypass_invert_chk` |TDO is the bitwise inverse of TDI, delayed by one TCK cycle
|`inv_bypass_capture_chk` |Capture-DR loads the inverted default (1) into the bypass register
|`inv_bypass_no_side_effect_chk` |IDCODE reads its identification after the inverted-bypass scans (`CHK-IDCODE-RAW`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: INV_BYPASS yields a 1-bit register producing the bitwise-inverted, one-cycle-delayed TDI with an inverted default capture, and IDCODE reads its identification afterwards, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-013-dtp_jtag_trst_test]]
===== 0.13 `dtp_jtag_trst_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: TRST asynchronous TAP reset

*Test Coverage*:

* Asynchronous TRST_N forces the TAP to Test-Logic-Reset from any state, independent of TCK
* `jtag_ptap_state_o` is Test-Logic-Reset as soon as TRST_N is low, before any TCK edge, and stays there through TCK cycles with TMS low, which would leave Test-Logic-Reset were the reset not holding the controller
* IR resets to IDCODE on TRST and scan state is cleared
* Normal operation resumes cleanly after TRST_N deassertion (Run-Test/Idle, IDCODE readable)
* `cg_tap_smoke` `trst` reset-source bin, the `cp_trst_from_state` bin of every TAP state other than Test-Logic-Reset, and the IDCODE read after TRST
* Power-on reset path: `pwr_on_rst_ni` forces the TAP to TLR independent of TRST, confirming `jtag_trst_n = pwr_on_rst_ni AND trst_n` (either source resets the TAP); proven by `dtp_jtag_trst_por_independence_test` (0.13a)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive the TAP into a state other than Test-Logic-Reset via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`, with a seeded instruction other than IDCODE loaded (BYPASS 0x00, BYPASS 0x3F, or SAMPLE/PRELOAD). Each pass takes all fifteen such states in a seeded order and runs the steps below from each.
. Assert TRST_N and sample `jtag_ptap_state_o` before any TCK edge: Test-Logic-Reset.
. Hold TRST_N low for 2..8 TCK cycles with TMS low and confirm the TAP stays in Test-Logic-Reset.
. Release TRST_N; one TMS-low step reaches Run-Test/Idle.
. Perform a 32-bit DR scan with no IR load and confirm it reads IDCODE: the reset selected the device-identification register. The path to Update-IR is a Capture-IR to Update-IR scan with no Shift-IR, which itself selects IDCODE, so from that state the read confirms recovery only and the reset is judged by steps 2 and 3.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`trst_to_tlr_chk` |TAP is in TLR as soon as TRST_N is low, before any TCK edge (`CHK-TAP-TRST-ASYNC`), and stays there through TMS-low TCK cycles under the reset (`CHK-TAP-TRST-TLR`)
|`trst_ir_idcode_chk` |After release a DR scan with no IR load reads IDCODE: the reset selected the device-identification register (`CHK-IDCODE-RAW`)
|`trst_recovery_chk` |One TMS-low step after release reaches Run-Test/Idle and the DR scan completes (`CHK-TAP-STATE`, `CHK-IDCODE-RAW`)
|`por_to_tlr_chk` |`pwr_on_rst_ni` forces TLR (IR=IDCODE) independent of TRST, per `jtag_trst_n = pwr_on_rst_ni AND trst_n`; proven by 0.13a (`CHK-TAP-POR-TLR`, `CHK-IDCODE-RECOVERY`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Asserting TRST_N from any state puts the TAP in TLR before any TCK edge and holds it there under TMS-low clocks, and after release a TMS-low step and a DR scan with no IR load read IDCODE, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-013a-dtp_jtag_trst_por_independence_test]]
===== 0.13a `dtp_jtag_trst_por_independence_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: POR-only TAP reset with TRST held high

*Test Coverage*:

* `pwr_on_rst_ni` independently forces the primary TAP to Test-Logic-Reset while `trst_n` remains deasserted and TCK idles
* IR reloads IDCODE after POR without any TRST activity or JTAG BFM reset
* Normal TAP operation resumes after POR release: one TMS-low step reaches Run-Test/Idle and a DR scan with no IR load reads IDCODE
* `cg_tap_smoke` `por` reset-source bin and the IDCODE read after power-on reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Hold `trst_n` high and drive the TAP into a seeded non-reset state with a seeded instruction other than IDCODE loaded (BYPASS 0x00, BYPASS 0x3F, or SAMPLE/PRELOAD).
. Hold `pwr_on_rst_ni` low for 2..8 TCK periods with TCK idle; sample `jtag_ptap_state_o` (Test-Logic-Reset) and `trst_n` (high) during the pulse; release it.
. Step TLR -++>++ Run-Test/Idle by one TMS-low cycle.
. Perform a 32-bit DR scan with no IR load and no TRST activity; it reads IDCODE.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`por_independent_tlr_chk` |`jtag_ptap_state_o` is Test-Logic-Reset during the POR pulse with `trst_n` sampled high and no TCK edge (`CHK-TAP-POR-TLR`), and the tb_top assertion counter advanced across the pulse (`CHK-RESET-COUNT`)
|`por_idcode_reload_chk` |The DR scan after release, with no IR load, reads IDCODE: POR reloaded the device-identification register (`CHK-IDCODE-RECOVERY`)
|`por_recovery_scan_chk` |TLR -++>++ RTI by one TMS-low step and the DR scan complete without any TRST activity (`CHK-TAP-STATE`, `CHK-IDCODE-RECOVERY`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: POR alone resets the TAP to TLR with TRST high, reloads IDCODE, and a TMS-low step plus a DR scan with no IR load read it back, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-014-dtp_jtag_tlr_reset_test]]
===== 0.14 `dtp_jtag_tlr_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Test-Logic-Reset behavior

*Test Coverage*:

* Entering Test-Logic-Reset (5x TMS=1) resets the instruction register to IDCODE
* Non-hold-protected TDRs return to their defaults: DEBUG_CONTROL to 0x00 and IC_RESET written with `reset_hold` = 1 to its all-ones default, with the clock-stop, boot-stall, and IC_RESET slice pin outputs following
* TDRs written with `reset_hold` = 0 keep their values through TLR (`dtp_jtag_ic_reset_test`), and the 3DCR `config_hold` behavior is `dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test`
* Normal IR/DR access resumes after stepping TLR -++>++ Run-Test/Idle
* `cg_tap_smoke` `tms5` reset-source bin and the IDCODE read after a TMS reset, the `cg_ic_reset` `tlr_restore` bin, and the `cg_debug_control` `tlr_cleared` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Write DEBUG_CONTROL (boot stall, its override, and the CLA clock-stop enable set) and IC_RESET (`reset_hold` = 1, every slice enable active) to non-default images through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and confirm the pin outputs follow them; load BYPASS.
. Drive the TAP to Test-Logic-Reset via 5..8 TMS=1 cycles from a seeded start state.
. Perform a 32-bit DR scan with no IR load and confirm it reads IDCODE.
. Verify the pin outputs are back at their reset values, DEBUG_CONTROL reads 0x00, and IC_RESET reads its all-ones default.
. Load IDCODE by IR scan and read it back: normal IR/DR access has resumed.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tlr_ir_idcode_chk` |IR loads IDCODE on entering TLR (`CHK-TAP-TLR-TMS5`, `CHK-TAP-TLR-IDCODE`)
|`tlr_tdr_default_chk` |After TLR DEBUG_CONTROL reads 0x00 and IC_RESET (written with `reset_hold` = 1) its all-ones default, with the stop_clks, cla_clock_stop_en, boot-stall, and IC_RESET slice pin outputs at their reset values; the programmed images are confirmed on the pins before the walk (`CHK-TLR-TDR-DEFAULT`)
|`tlr_resume_chk` |IDCODE loaded by an IR scan after the TMS-driven TLR reads back through a DR scan (`CHK-TLR-RESUME`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Entering TLR resets IR to IDCODE and returns DEBUG_CONTROL and the non-held IC_RESET image to their defaults on the TDRs and the pins while IR/DR access resumes, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-015-dtp_jtag_clamp_hold_test]]
===== 0.15 `dtp_jtag_clamp_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: CLAMP_HOLD TMP

*Test Coverage*:

* CLAMP_HOLD (IR=0x0A) TMP instruction turns test-mode persistence on at its Update-IR
* CLAMP_HOLD's data register is the one-bit bypass (its DR scan returns TDI one TCK late); the 2-bit register is TMP_STATUS, whose bit 1 reads the persistence back
* Persistence survives an instruction switch to BYPASS and five TMS-high clocks into Test-Logic-Reset; CLAMP_RELEASE clears it, and so does a TRST reset
* `chrst_n`, the reset that holds the boundary register through Test-Logic-Reset while persistence is on, is observed on the exported `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.chrst_n` by `dtp_jtag_tmp_status_chrst_n_in_persistence_test` (1.2, `CHK-TMP-CHRST`); the bench has no boundary cells, so clamped pins are not observable
* `cg_dr_scan` `clamp_hold` bin and the `cg_tmp` `clamp_hold_on`, `unarmed_held`, `persistence_on_released`, and `tap_reset_off` bins

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Shift a directed or seeded pattern through the looped-back chain under SAMPLE/PRELOAD.
. Read TMP_STATUS: persistence 0. Load CLAMP_HOLD (IR=0x0A) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; confirm its decode and that a 64-bit DR scan returns TDI one TCK late.
. Read TMP_STATUS and confirm bit 1 (persistence) is 1.
. Load BYPASS and read TMP_STATUS again: persistence 1.
. Load CLAMP_RELEASE and read TMP_STATUS: persistence 0. After the sweep, engage CLAMP_HOLD again, walk five TMS-high clocks into Test-Logic-Reset and confirm persistence 1, then reset the TAP with TRST and confirm persistence 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`clamp_hold_dr_len_chk` |CLAMP_HOLD decodes as itself and scans the one-bit bypass register, TDI one TCK late; the 2-bit register is TMP_STATUS, read for the persistence bit (`CHK-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`clamp_hold_persist_chk` |Persistence survives an instruction switch to BYPASS and clears on CLAMP_RELEASE (`CHK-TMP-PERSIST`); `chrst_n`, released in Test-Logic-Reset while persistence is on, is observed by `dtp_jtag_tmp_status_chrst_n_in_persistence_test` (1.2)
|`clamp_hold_tmp_status_chk` |TMP_STATUS bit 1 reads 0 before CLAMP_HOLD and 1 after it, 1 after a TMS-driven Test-Logic-Reset, 0 after CLAMP_RELEASE, and 0 after a TRST reset from Persistence-On (`CHK-TMP-PERSIST`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CLAMP_HOLD decodes, scans the one-bit bypass, and turns persistence on, which BYPASS and a TMS-driven Test-Logic-Reset preserve and CLAMP_RELEASE or TRST clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-016-dtp_jtag_clamp_release_test]]
===== 0.16 `dtp_jtag_clamp_release_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: CLAMP_RELEASE TMP

*Test Coverage*:

* CLAMP_RELEASE (IR=0x0B) TMP instruction releases the persistent clamp state
* CLAMP_RELEASE transitions TMP from Persistence-On to Persistence-Off; like CLAMP_HOLD's, its data register is the one-bit bypass (its DR scan returns TDI one TCK late), and the 2-bit register is TMP_STATUS
* TMP_STATUS transitions 1 -++>++ 0 after CLAMP_RELEASE
* After the release, SAMPLE/PRELOAD selects the looped-back chain again: its select is high across the DR scan while the TAP strobes pulse, and the scan returns its pattern
* `cg_dr_scan` `clamp_release` bin and the `cg_tmp` `clamp_release_off` and `unarmed_held` bins

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Engage CLAMP_HOLD (per the 0.15 setup) and confirm TMP_STATUS.persistence = 1.
. Load CLAMP_RELEASE (IR=0x0B) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and perform a DR scan; TDI returns on TDO one TCK late.
. Confirm the TMP controller exits Persistence-On.
. Read TMP_STATUS and verify TMP_STATUS = 0.
. Load SAMPLE/PRELOAD and scan under a scan-control window: the chain select is high across the scan and the pattern loops back one TCK late.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`clamp_release_dr_len_chk` |CLAMP_RELEASE decodes as itself and scans the one-bit bypass register, TDI one TCK late (`CHK-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|`clamp_release_tmp_status_chk` |TMP_STATUS transitions from 1 to 0 after CLAMP_RELEASE (`CHK-TMP-PERSIST`)
|`clamp_release_chain_chk` |SAMPLE/PRELOAD after the release selects the chain and returns the looped-back pattern (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CLAMP_RELEASE clears persistence and scans the one-bit bypass, drives TMP_STATUS 1-++>++0, and the chain scans again after the release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-017-dtp_jtag_ac_extest_train_test]]
===== 0.17 `dtp_jtag_ac_extest_train_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: EXTEST_TRAIN

*Test Coverage*:

* EXTEST_TRAIN (IR=0x05) selects the boundary-scan chain; the bench has no AC boundary cells and loops the chain back, so a DR scan returns the pattern one TCK late
* The chain's `run_test_idle` scan-control field, the strobe the AC training launches on, is high while the TAP is parked in Run-Test/Idle with EXTEST_TRAIN loaded, low in Test-Logic-Reset, and low across a DR scan until the scan returns to Run-Test/Idle
* Across an EXTEST_TRAIN DR scan the select is high with one capture, N shift, and one update strobe; under BYPASS the select stays low while the strobes pulse
* `cg_jtag_instruction` EXTEST_TRAIN opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `extest_train` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SAMPLE/PRELOAD and shift a zero preload through the looped-back chain.
. Load EXTEST_TRAIN (IR=0x05) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and shift directed, train, and seeded patterns; each returns retimed by one TCK.
. Park in Run-Test/Idle with EXTEST_TRAIN loaded: `run_test_idle` high, `test_logic_reset` low, select low. Walk to Test-Logic-Reset: `run_test_idle` low, `test_logic_reset` high. Return to Run-Test/Idle.
. Open a scan-control window across an EXTEST_TRAIN DR scan: select high, capture once, shift N times, update once, and `run_test_idle` low until the return to Run-Test/Idle.
. Load BYPASS: the window shows select low while the strobes pulse. Exit through an EXTEST loopback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`extest_train_pattern_chk` |The training launch strobe (`run_test_idle`) is high while parked in Run-Test/Idle with EXTEST_TRAIN loaded and low in Test-Logic-Reset, and the shifted pattern returns retimed by one TCK; the bench has no AC boundary cells, so the driven pattern itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-RTI`, `CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|`extest_train_timing_chk` |Across an EXTEST_TRAIN DR scan the select is high with one capture, N shift, and one update strobe, and `run_test_idle` stays low across the scan and is high again on the return to Run-Test/Idle (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BSR-RTI`)
|`extest_train_no_disturb_chk` |Under BYPASS the select stays low across a DR scan while the strobes pulse, so no boundary update can occur outside the training instruction; the bench has no boundary cells, so pin state itself is not observable (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`, `CHK-BYPASS-DELAY`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: EXTEST_TRAIN selects the looped-back chain, its `run_test_idle` launch strobe follows the Run-Test/Idle state, low across each scan and high on its return, and the scan controls carry the capture, shift, and update strobes of an N-bit scan, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-018-dtp_jtag_ac_extest_pulse_test]]
===== 0.18 `dtp_jtag_ac_extest_pulse_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local BSR model

*Objective*: EXTEST_PULSE

*Test Coverage*:

* EXTEST_PULSE (IR=0x06) decodes as itself and selects the boundary-scan chain, which this bench loops back without boundary cells
* The chain's run_test_idle strobe, the Run-Test/Idle decode the AC pulse launches on, is low across an EXTEST_PULSE scan until the scan returns to Run-Test/Idle, and high while the TAP is parked there
* The chain select rides the TAP's capture, shift, and update strobes across each scan
* Captured response bits are the looped-back pattern retimed by one TCK
* `cg_jtag_instruction` EXTEST_PULSE opcode bin and the `cg_dr_scan` `extest_pulse` bin

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load EXTEST_TRAIN and preload a pattern through the looped-back chain.
. Load EXTEST_PULSE (IR=0x06) through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and scan directed, walking-one, and edge patterns: each returns one TCK late.
. Scan a seeded pattern under a scan-control window: the chain select is high across the scan, the capture and update strobes pulse once and the shift strobe N times, and run_test_idle stays low until the return to Run-Test/Idle.
. Parked in Run-Test/Idle, confirm run_test_idle is high and the chain select low.
. Load BYPASS and scan: the select stays low while the same strobes pulse.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`extest_pulse_select_chk` |EXTEST_PULSE selects the chain: select high across the scan with the TAP strobes (`CHK-BSR-SELECT`, `CHK-BSR-SCAN-CTRL`)
|`extest_pulse_rti_chk` |run_test_idle stays low across the scan, is high again on the return to Run-Test/Idle, and holds while the TAP is parked there (`CHK-BSR-RTI`)
|`extest_pulse_capture_chk` |Captured response bits are the looped-back pattern retimed by one TCK (`CHK-BSR-LOOPBACK`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: EXTEST_PULSE selects the chain with the TAP strobes on each scan, raises run_test_idle on the Run-Test/Idle return and while parked, and returns each looped-back pattern, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-1-tmp-debug-control-and-capabilities]]
==== 1. TMP, Debug Control, and Capabilities

[[sec-11-dtp_jtag_tmp_status_register_smoke_test]]
===== 1.1 `dtp_jtag_tmp_status_register_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: TMP_STATUS reset/read smoke

*Test Coverage*:

* TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C) 2-bit TDR selectable and shiftable on the primary TAP
* TMP_STATUS[1] reads 0 out of reset (no persistence active)
* TMP_STATUS accurately reflects the TMP FSM state when CLAMP_HOLD engages Persistence-On
* TMP_STATUS reads are repeated with multiple caller-selected DR shift values to expose unintended read side effects
* A following IDCODE read returns the configured IDCODE

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the primary TAP and read TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C).
. Verify TMP_STATUS[1] = 0 (no persistence active out of reset).
. Re-read TMP_STATUS with 2-bit shift-in values `0b00`, `0b01`, `0b10`, and `0b11`; verify persistence remains 0.
. Engage CLAMP_HOLD to drive the TMP FSM into Persistence-On.
. Re-select TMP_STATUS and verify TMP_STATUS[1] = 1.
. Read IDCODE afterward and verify the configured IDCODE.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tmp_status_reset_chk` |TMP_STATUS[1] reads 0 out of reset
|`tmp_status_shift_value_chk` |Multiple TMP_STATUS read shift values do not disturb persistence
|`tmp_status_fsm_track_chk` |TMP_STATUS[1] tracks the TMP FSM Persistence-On state
|`tmp_status_side_effect_chk` |A following IDCODE read returns the configured IDCODE (`CHK-DBG-TDR`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: TMP_STATUS reads its reset value and tracks the TMP FSM state across the persistence transition, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-12-dtp_jtag_tmp_status_chrst_n_in_persistence_test]]
===== 1.2 `dtp_jtag_tmp_status_chrst_n_in_persistence_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: TMP persistence across the system reset and Test-Logic-Reset

*Test Coverage*:

* TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C) Persistence-On state established through CLAMP_HOLD
* Persistence survives a system-reset (`rst_n_i`) pulse, which resets the cross-trigger and AXI logic and not the TAP controller, while TAP access is preserved
* With persistence on, Test-Logic-Reset entered by five TMS-high clocks leaves the TMP conditional reset released on the boundary-scan host control (`jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.chrst_n` = 1) and TMP_STATUS[1] set; with persistence off, Test-Logic-Reset asserts it
* A following IDCODE read returns the configured IDCODE

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and engage CLAMP_HOLD to enter Persistence-On.
. Read TMP_STATUS and confirm TMP_STATUS[1] = 1 before the reset.
. Pulse the system reset `rst_n_i` for a seeded number of `clk_i` cycles while keeping the primary TAP accessible; the tb_top reset-assertion counter advances by one.
. Re-select TMP_STATUS and re-read the 2-bit TDR.
a. Verify TMP_STATUS[1] remains 1, proving persistence bits that must survive the system reset are preserved.
. Drive five TMS-high clocks to Test-Logic-Reset without TRST.
a. Verify `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.test_logic_reset` = 1 and `chrst_n` = 1.
. Return to Run-Test/Idle and verify TMP_STATUS[1] = 1.
. Load CLAMP_RELEASE, verify TMP_STATUS[1] = 0, and drive Test-Logic-Reset again.
a. Verify `test_logic_reset` = 1 and `chrst_n` = 0 in Test-Logic-Reset, and `chrst_n` = 1 after the return to Run-Test/Idle.
. Read IDCODE and verify the configured IDCODE.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tmp_persistence_set_chk` |Persistence-On established (TMP_STATUS[1] = 1) before reset
|`tmp_persistence_chrst_chk` |TMP_STATUS[1] preserved across the `rst_n_i` pulse, which `CHK-RESET-COUNT` counts, and across Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-TMP-PERSIST`); `chrst_n` released in Test-Logic-Reset while persistence is on and asserted there while it is off (`CHK-TMP-CHRST`)
|`tmp_persistence_side_effect_chk` |A following IDCODE read returns the configured IDCODE (`CHK-DBG-TDR`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Persistence established through CLAMP_HOLD survives the system-reset pulse and Test-Logic-Reset, and `chrst_n` follows persistence in Test-Logic-Reset, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-13-dtp_jtag_tmp_status_bypass_escape_test]]
===== 1.3 `dtp_jtag_tmp_status_bypass_escape_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: BYPASS_ESCAPE behavior

*Test Coverage*:

* TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C) DR write of BYPASS_ESCAPE[0] = 1
* BYPASS instruction triggers TMP FSM exit from Persistence-On to Persistence-Off when BYPASS_ESCAPE is armed
* TMP_STATUS[1] returns to 0 after the escape path completes
* Scan routing exits cleanly back to normal operation after the BYPASS escape
* A TAP reset clears an armed BYPASS_ESCAPE[0], and TMP_STATUS reads it back as 0

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Engage CLAMP_HOLD via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` to place the TMP FSM in Persistence-On; confirm TMP_STATUS[1] = 1.
. Load TMP_STATUS (IR=0x0C) and write BYPASS_ESCAPE[0] = 1 through the TMP_STATUS DR; Update-DR.
. Read TMP_STATUS with preserve values that keep BYPASS_ESCAPE armed; verify BYPASS_ESCAPE = 1 and TMP_STATUS[1] = 1 on each read.
. Load BYPASS twice to invoke the escape path; the second Update-IR observes BYPASS already selected.
a. Verify the TMP FSM transitions from Persistence-On to Persistence-Off.
. Re-select TMP_STATUS and read the 2-bit TDR.
a. Verify TMP_STATUS[1] = 0 after the escape.
. Confirm scan routing returns to normal with a BYPASS scan pattern.
. With persistence off, write BYPASS_ESCAPE[0] = 1, reset the TAP, and verify TMP_STATUS reads 0b00.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`bypass_escape_arm_chk` |BYPASS_ESCAPE[0] write read back from the TMP_STATUS DR, with TMP_STATUS[1] = 1 on every armed read, and 0 after a TAP reset that follows an arm write
|`bypass_escape_exit_chk` |BYPASS instruction drives Persistence-Off; TMP_STATUS[1] clears to 0
|`bypass_escape_side_effect_chk` |Scan routing returns to normal with no unintended TDR side effects
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Arming BYPASS_ESCAPE and loading BYPASS forces the TMP FSM out of persistence and TMP_STATUS reads 0, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-14-dtp_jtag_ic_reset_test]]
===== 1.4 `dtp_jtag_ic_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: IC_RESET override control

*Test Coverage*:

* IC_RESET (IR=0x0D) TDR independently overrides each chiplet reset domain across three slices — SMC (nearest TDI), SEP, and External (nearest TDO) — all enabled in this bench (`IcResetSmcEnable`, `IcResetSepEnable`, `IcResetExtEnable` in `tb/dtp_dv_cfg_pkg.sv`), one port each
* Active-low `reset_enable` per IEEE 1149.1 §17: `reset_enable=1` (default) disables the override (upstream reset passes through), `reset_enable=0` enables it; the PTAP exports `jtag_ic_reset_ovrd_o[n] = !reset_enable[n]` and `jtag_ic_reset_ctrl_n_o[n] = reset_control[n]`
* RESET_HOLD = 0 keeps every reset_enable and reset_control bit across TLR (PTAP document, "PTAP IC_RESET fields" table)
* Four directed reset_hold=0 patterns give every slice each of its four reset_enable/reset_control values
* RESET_HOLD = 1 allows TLR to restore the default all-ones value; TRST always restores defaults
* Seeded random reset_enable/reset_control patterns exercise reset_hold=0 persistence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load IC_RESET (IR=0x0D) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Verify the default all-ones IC_RESET value and deasserted flattened SMC/SEP/EXT outputs.
. For each SMC, SEP, and External slice, shift in RESET_ENABLE=0 (enable override) and RESET_CONTROL=0 (drive reset low); Update-DR.
a. Verify `jtag_ic_reset_ovrd_o[0]` = 1 (= `!reset_enable[0]`) and `jtag_ic_reset_ctrl_n_o[0]` = 0.
b. Set RESET_ENABLE[0]=1 (disable override); verify `jtag_ic_reset_ovrd_o[0]` = 0 (upstream reset passes through), confirming the active-low `ovrd_o = !reset_enable` mapping.
. For each of four directed patterns, in which the SMC, SEP, and External slices take successive (reset_enable, reset_control) values of 00, 01, 10, and 11 so that every slice takes all four: set RESET_HOLD=0 with the pattern, Update-DR, force TLR (Test-Logic-Reset), and re-read IC_RESET.
a. Verify the programmed pattern is preserved on the slice outputs while the TAP is in Test-Logic-Reset, and in the readback.
. Repeat reset_hold=0 TLR preservation with seeded random enable/control patterns.
. Set RESET_HOLD=1 with a non-default pattern; force TLR and verify the override outputs deassert while the TAP is in Test-Logic-Reset and IC_RESET returns to its default all-ones value.
. Program a non-default reset_hold=0 pattern, assert TRST, and verify the override outputs deassert and IC_RESET returns to defaults.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ic_reset_polarity_chk` |`jtag_ic_reset_ovrd_o[n]` == `!reset_enable[n]` (active-low enable per 1149.1 §17); `jtag_ic_reset_ctrl_n_o[n]` == `reset_control[n]`
|`ic_reset_slice_chk` |Each slice (SMC/SEP/EXT) overrides independently of the other two
|`ic_reset_hold_chk` |RESET_HOLD=0 preserves the register and the slice outputs across TLR; RESET_HOLD=1 lets TLR restore defaults and deassert the outputs; TRST restores defaults; the outputs are judged before the readback, whose Update-DR writes the expected value back
|`ic_reset_random_hold_chk` |Seeded random reset_hold=0 enable/control patterns preserve across TLR, on the slice outputs and in the readback
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each slice (SMC/SEP/EXT) is independently overridden with the active-low `reset_enable` correctly mapped to `ovrd_o = !reset_enable`, and RESET_HOLD correctly controls TLR preservation/default restore behavior, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-15-dtp_dbg_jtag_caps_test]]
===== 1.5 `dtp_dbg_jtag_caps_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: JTAG_CAPS readback

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG_CAPS (IR=0x19) 60-bit read-only TDR (fields span `[59:0]`) reports compile-time parameter values
* Full-layout per-field checks: NUM_XTRIG_INT_CT `[59:54]`, NUM_XTRIG_CTP `[53:48]`, NUM_XTRA_STAP `[47:44]`, STAP_IO_EN/SEP_DBG_EN/SMC_DBG_EN `[43:41]`, NUM_SMC_IC_RST `[40:33]`, NUM_SEP_IC_RST `[32:25]`, NUM_EXT_IC_RST `[24:17]`, IC_RST_INST_EN `[16]`, instruction-enable bits `[15:8]`, OCH_VER `[7:0]`
* Repeated reads return a stable value
* Register is read-only across all-zeros, all-ones, alternating, and seeded-random write-attempt patterns
* CAPS read remains stable after switching through IDCODE and BYPASS instructions

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load JTAG_CAPS (IR=0x19) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Capture-DR / Shift-DR the full 60-bit TDR (`[59:0]`).
. Compare each field against its compile-time parameter:
a. NUM_XTRIG_INT_CT == `XTRIG_NUM_INT_CT`; NUM_XTRIG_CTP == `XTRIG_NUM_CTP`.
b. NUM_XTRA_STAP == `JTAG_NUM_EXTRA_STAPS`; STAP_IO_EN/SEP_DBG_EN/SMC_DBG_EN == their enable parameters.
c. NUM_SMC_IC_RST / NUM_SEP_IC_RST / NUM_EXT_IC_RST == their per-slice IC_RESET width parameters.
d. IC_RST_INST_EN and all instruction-enable bits `[15:8]` == their parameters; OCH_VER == `JTAG_OCH_VER`.
. Read the register multiple times and verify all reads match.
. Attempt multiple write patterns (Shift-DR new data {plus} Update-DR), then re-read after each attempt.
a. Verify the CAPS value is unchanged (read-only).
. Switch through IDCODE and BYPASS, then re-read JTAG_CAPS and verify it is unchanged.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`jtag_caps_fields_chk` |Every JTAG_CAPS field across `[59:0]` matches its compile-time parameter
|`jtag_caps_multi_read_chk` |Repeated reads are stable
|`jtag_caps_ro_chk` |Multiple write-attempt patterns leave CAPS value unchanged
|`jtag_caps_side_effect_chk` |CAPS read remains stable after instruction switches
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All JTAG_CAPS fields in the full `[59:0]` layout, including the per-slice IC_RESET widths, match their parameters and the register rejects writes, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-16-dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_jtag_clock_stop_test]]
===== 1.6 `dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_jtag_clock_stop_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: JTAG clock-stop output

*Test Coverage*:

* DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) 5-bit TDR reset value = 0x00
* JTAG_CLOCK_STOP bit folds into the CTN halt path to drive `stop_clks_o`
* `stop_clks_o` is the halt output registered in `clk_i`: checks poll until it settles, and out of system reset no change of it falls outside a `clk_i` rising edge
* No CLA request driven in this scenario, so `stop_clks_o` reflects the JTAG path only

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; read the 5-bit TDR and verify the reset value = 0x00.
. Write JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=1; Update-DR.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 1 (JTAG clock stop folds into the CTN functional halt).
. Read DEBUG_CONTROL with a preserve shift value and verify JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=1 while CLA_CLOCK_STOP=0.
. Write JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=0; Update-DR.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 0 (clocks released).
. Confirm `cla_clock_stop_en_o` is unaffected and no CLA request was asserted during the scenario.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`dbg_ctrl_reset_chk` |DEBUG_CONTROL reads 0x00 out of reset
|`jtag_clock_stop_chk` |`stop_clks_o` tracks JTAG_CLOCK_STOP via the CTN halt path
|`jtag_clock_stop_release_chk` |`stop_clks_o` deasserts when JTAG_CLOCK_STOP clears
|`jtag_clock_stop_registered_chk` |No `stop_clks_o` change during the pass falls outside a `clk_i` rising edge (`CHK-DBG-STOP-EDGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: `stop_clks_o` asserts and releases with the JTAG_CLOCK_STOP bit through the CTN halt path, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-17-dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_cla_clock_stop_test]]
===== 1.7 `dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_cla_clock_stop_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: CLA clock-stop request/readback

*Test Coverage*:

* DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN bit exported as `cla_clock_stop_en_o` (does not gate `stop_clks_o`)
* CLA-driven stop via `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i` OR-combines in the CTN halt to assert `stop_clks_o`
* CLA_CLOCK_STOP readback in DEBUG_CONTROL reflects CLA requests only (not the JTAG path)
* Deasserting the CLA request releases `stop_clks_o` and clears the readback; out of system reset, `stop_clks_o` changes only at `clk_i` rising edges

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; verify the reset value = 0x00.
. Write CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN=1; Update-DR.
a. Verify `cla_clock_stop_en_o` = 1 (exported; does not itself gate `stop_clks_o`).
. Drive `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i[0]` = 1 alone from the TB.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 1 (CTN OR of CLA requests).
. Read DEBUG_CONTROL and verify CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 1 (readback reflects CLA OR only).
. Deassert the requests.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 0; re-read DEBUG_CONTROL and verify CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 0; repeat steps 3 to 5 with a seeded nonzero request mask.
. Write CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN=0; Update-DR; verify `cla_clock_stop_en_o` = 0.
. With DEBUG_CONTROL cleared, drive a seeded nonzero request mask.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 1, `cla_clock_stop_en_o` = 0, and CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 1 with CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN = 0 in the readback; release the requests and verify `stop_clks_o` = 0 and CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 0.
. Write JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=1 with no CLA request; read DEBUG_CONTROL with a preserve value that keeps JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=1 throughout this step.
a. Verify `stop_clks_o` = 1 and the readback JTAG_CLOCK_STOP = 1, CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 0.
b. Drive a seeded nonzero request mask and verify CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 1; release it and verify CLA_CLOCK_STOP = 0 while `stop_clks_o` stays 1.
c. Write JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=0 and verify `stop_clks_o` = 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`cla_clock_stop_en_chk` |`cla_clock_stop_en_o` tracks CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN, and a CLA request with CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN=0 asserts `stop_clks_o`
|`cla_clock_stop_req_chk` |`stop_clks_o` follows `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i` through the CTN OR path
|`cla_clock_stop_readback_chk` |DEBUG_CONTROL CLA_CLOCK_STOP reads the OR of the CLA requests under either enable value, and 0 with JTAG_CLOCK_STOP=1 and no request
|`cla_clock_stop_registered_chk` |No `stop_clks_o` change during the pass falls outside a `clk_i` rising edge (`CHK-DBG-STOP-EDGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA request drives `stop_clks_o` through the CTN OR while `cla_clock_stop_en_o` is exported independently and the readback tracks CLA requests, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-18-dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_random_clock_stop_test]]
===== 1.8 `dtp_dbg_ctrl_clk_stop_random_clock_stop_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Random clock-stop combinations

*Test Coverage*:

* Directed and seeded-random DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) JTAG_CLOCK_STOP / CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN combinations crossed with `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i`
* `stop_clks_o` = registered(`jtag_clock_stop` | `|xtrig_clk_stop_req_i`) across all legal combinations
* `stop_clks_o` is a `clk_i` register: out of system reset, with every CLA request change presented at a `clk_i` falling edge, `stop_clks_o` changes only at `clk_i` rising edges, once per change of the request OR
* `cla_clock_stop_en_o` tracks CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN independently and never gates `stop_clks_o`
* CLA_CLOCK_STOP readback reflects CLA requests only across the randomized stimulus
* Per-source aggregation: each of the up-to-`NUM_CLK_STOP_REQ` (default 9) `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i[i]` inputs individually drives the OR-reduction into `cla_clock_stop`/`stop_clks_o`, plus combinations of multiple simultaneous request lines

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With DEBUG_CONTROL cleared after the TAP reset, drive a seeded walk of `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i` vectors (a nonzero mask, zero, one seeded line, zero, two different nonzero masks, zero), holding each until `stop_clks_o` settles.
. Generate legal combinations of JTAG_CLOCK_STOP, CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN, and `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i` vectors, including a directed pass that walks a single `1` across each `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i[i]` (`0..NUM_CLK_STOP_REQ-1`), multi-request masks, and seeded random request/control combinations.
. For each combination, write DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and drive the corresponding `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i`; Update-DR.
. Sample `stop_clks_o`, `cla_clock_stop_en_o`, and the DEBUG_CONTROL readback fields.
. Compare against the bit-accurate reference model:
a. `stop_clks_o` == registered(`jtag_clock_stop` | OR of `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i`).
b. `cla_clock_stop_en_o` == CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN (no gating of `stop_clks_o`).
c. CLA_CLOCK_STOP readback == OR of `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i` only.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`clk_stop_random_halt_chk` |`stop_clks_o` matches registered(`jtag_clock_stop` OR reduction of `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i`) per combination
|`clk_stop_random_en_export_chk` |`cla_clock_stop_en_o` tracks the TDR bit and never gates `stop_clks_o`
|`clk_stop_random_readback_chk` |CLA_CLOCK_STOP readback reflects CLA requests only
|`clk_stop_per_source_chk` |Each individual `xtrig_clk_stop_req_i[i]` and multi-line combinations OR-reduce into `cla_clock_stop`/`stop_clks_o`
|`clk_stop_registered_chk` |`stop_clks_o` changes once per change of the request OR across the walk, and never outside a `clk_i` rising edge during the pass (`CHK-DBG-STOP-EDGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Across all randomized and per-source combinations the halt output, exported enable, and CLA readback match the bit-accurate reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-19-dtp_dbg_ctrl_boot_stall_test]]
===== 1.9 `dtp_dbg_ctrl_boot_stall_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Boot-stall override

*Test Coverage*:

* DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL fields
* All four BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL combinations are written and read back
* Every BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL combination remains independent of each nonzero JTAG_CLOCK_STOP / CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN combination
* TDR reset value all-zeros (0x00), read after reset and after a TAP reset that follows a seeded nonzero write

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load DEBUG_CONTROL (IR=0x18) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; read the 5-bit TDR and verify reset value 0x00.
. Loop all four BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL combinations and verify both flattened outputs plus DEBUG_CONTROL readback.
. Keep BOOT_STALL_OVRD asserted and toggle BOOT_STALL through multiple values to confirm the data field changes independently.
. For each BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL combination, set each of the three nonzero JTAG_CLOCK_STOP / CLA_CLOCK_STOP_EN combinations; verify the boot-stall outputs and the readback of all four fields.
. Write a seeded nonzero value to DEBUG_CONTROL[3:0], reset the TAP, and verify both boot-stall outputs deassert and DEBUG_CONTROL reads 0x00.
. Clear DEBUG_CONTROL and verify boot-stall outputs deassert.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`boot_stall_reset_chk` |DEBUG_CONTROL reads 0x00 out of reset, including after a TAP reset that follows a seeded nonzero write
|`boot_stall_combo_chk` |All four BOOT_STALL_OVRD / BOOT_STALL combinations drive `jtag_boot_stall_ovrd_o` and `jtag_boot_stall_o` to their field values; the consumer selects the stall with the override (PTAP document, DEBUG_CONTROL)
|`boot_stall_readback_chk` |DEBUG_CONTROL readback matches each programmed combination
|`boot_stall_interaction_chk` |Boot-stall fields are independent of clock-stop fields
|`boot_stall_clear_chk` |`jtag_boot_stall_ovrd_o` deasserts when BOOT_STALL_OVRD clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each boot-stall pin follows its DEBUG_CONTROL field, both stay independent of the clock-stop fields, and both deassert when the register is cleared, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

* CAPS read remains stable after switching through IDCODE and BYPASS instructions

[cols=",",options="header",]

|`smc_jtag2axi_caps_side_effect_chk` |CAPS read remains stable after instruction switches

*Pass Criteria*: All SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS fields match the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge parameters and the register rejects writes, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''
