[[sec-2-jtag2axi-write-scenarios]]
==== 2. JTAG2AXI Write Scenarios

NOTE: Every JTAG2AXI scenario in this section and in <<sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios>> and
<<sec-4-jtag2axi-error-backpressure-cdc-and-decode-scenarios>> opens each pass with the geometry gate of the JTAG2AXI base
sequence: it reads the three `*_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` TDRs and records `CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` for the bus type,
address size, and data size of each bridge against the DV geometry table. A DUT that publishes a
different geometry fails the pass before any bridge
request is packed with the table's field widths.

NOTE: Every scenario in this section and in <<sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios>> except the
security-gating scenarios, and every `*_error_single_*` and `*_error_series_*` scenario of
<<sec-4-jtag2axi-error-backpressure-cdc-and-decode-scenarios>>, records `CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ` for each
bridge operation it issues, single operations and series beats alike, faulted or not: once the
operation completes, the port has completed exactly one single-beat transaction of its direction, at
the address the request carried over the full bridge address width, at its size on the AXI4 bridge,
and for a write with the strobes and strobed data the request places on the bus lanes; no other
transaction reaches the port between operations or between passes, and none reaches the other two
bridge ports. The responders store an access at its address modulo a 64 KiB window, so the series
and random-operation scenarios of this section and <<sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios>> and the seeded
single-write cases draw address bits above the window: those bits reach the bus, where this check
judges them, but not the responder memory.

[[sec-21-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test]]
===== 2.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single write

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op AXI4 write through the SMC fabric debug bridge (SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x28)
* 56-bit address / 64-bit data field formatting consistent with the SMC fabric CAPS TDR (IR=0x27, ADDR_SIZE=56, DATA_SIZE=64)
* Address/data/wstrb delivery to the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` / `axi_smc_dbg_resp_i`
* Op-status settles at OKAY (0b00) after the write completes; a poll that lands before completion reads Busy (0b11). OKAY is the all-zero encoding, so the responder burst count (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`) and the memory compare corroborate it
* A strobe sweep below the full beat at every size: no lane at 1, 2, 4, and 8 bytes, the top lane alone at 2, 4, and 8 bytes, the low half of a 4-byte beat, and `0x55` and `0xAA` at 8 bytes, beside the full-strobe write at each size and the seeded random cases

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load and verify the bridge CAPS:
a. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
b. Verify bus_type = AXI4, ADDR_SIZE = 56, DATA_SIZE = 64.
. Issue the single write:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in the target address, the write data, the wstrb of the case (all-ones, a strobe-sweep pattern, or a seeded one), the size, and op = 0b10 (Write); then Update-DR.
. Poll for completion:
a. Re-enter Capture-DR/Shift-DR and read the op-status field.
b. Repeat until status is no longer Busy (0b11).
c. Verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the side effect:
a. Confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate received exactly one AW/W beat at the target address with the expected data and wstrb (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).
b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4, ADDR_SIZE=56, DATA_SIZE=64
|`smc_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status settles at OKAY (0b00), corroborated by the responder burst count and the memory compare
|`smc_axi_wr_data_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The AXI4 single write completes with OKAY status and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate captures the correct address, data, and wstrb, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-21a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.1a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric single write delivers the expected data bytes, not only status/address fields
* Read-back through the same bridge verifies final memory-visible data and lane placement
* Write status remains OKAY and no unexpected AXI sideband error is observed

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SMC fabric single write with a non-trivial data pattern and full byte enables.
. Poll the op-status TDR until it returns OKAY and confirm the subordinate holds the pattern (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring beat the same way, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Issue a read to the same address through the SMC fabric bridge.
. Compare returned data against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written data pattern
|`smc_axi_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The written data is observable through bridge read-back at the same address with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-22-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) preloading the base address and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 64-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 64-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write to complete, confirm the port carried it at base{plus}i*stride with the beat's data (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`), and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word there (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes, one per beat, and no transaction during the SERIES_CTRL scans (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each beat puts exactly one write on the port, at base {plus} i*stride over the full 56-bit address
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-22a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_narrow_test]]
===== 2.2a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_narrow_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric 32-bit incrementing series write at beat offset +4

*Test Coverage*:

* A SERIES_DATA_INCR write stream with `size=2` (32-bit) whose base address is beat-aligned plus four bytes
* AXI WSTRB and WDATA placed on the lanes each beat's address selects within the 64-bit SMC fabric beat: `WSTRB=0xF0` with data in `[63:32]` at +4, `WSTRB=0x0F` with data in `[31:0]` at +0, alternating as the address advances
* Distinct 32-bit payload per address landing at `base+i*4` in the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate (no low-lane aliasing); the low word beside the first beat holds a sentinel the stream never targets
* Address advance of four bytes per beat, consistent with `AxSIZE=2`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat-aligned base with seeded address bits above the responder window, add four, set stride to four bytes, and choose a beat count N; in a seeded half of the passes the stream crosses the window top, carrying the series address into those bits.
b. Build a per-beat 32-bit data pattern, mirror each expected write in a reference memory at the size-aligned address, and preload a sentinel in the low word of the first beat's 64-bit slot.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with `op=Write`, `size=2`, and the +4 base; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: arm the strobe intent `0xF << (addr mod 8)` for `base+i*4`, then shift the i-th 32-bit payload through SERIES_DATA_INCR; Update-DR.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the corresponding AXI write to complete, confirm the responder holds the payload at `base+i*4` (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`), then capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status 0b00 and address `base+(i+1)*4`.
. Verify the stream footprint and the SERIES_CTRL capture:
a. Confirm the sentinel word and every beat's word are unchanged after the stream.
b. Confirm status is OKAY and the captured address equals `base+N*4`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_status_chk` |After each beat's write completes, the SERIES_CTRL capture holds the address `base+(i+1)*4` (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reports OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_lane_chk` |`CHK-AXI-STRB`: each observed write's strobes equal the stimulus intent `0xF << (addr mod 8)` (`0xF0` at +4, `0x0F` at +0), and the sentinel word beside the stream is unchanged
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_data_chk` |Each size-aligned address holds its distinct 32-bit payload, after its own beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`) and after the whole stream
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N 32-bit incrementing writes complete with OKAY status, drive the strobes of the address-selected AXI lanes, leave the sentinel untouched, and each target address holds its expected data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-23-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge all targeting one fixed address (no-incr mode)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) holding one address while SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write to complete and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, each at the fixed address over the full address width (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).
b. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each of the N beats puts exactly one write on the port, at the fixed address over the full address width
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds each beat's word after that beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`) and the final (last-beat) data at the end
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-24-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4 write series through the SMC fabric bridge carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.7>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`smc_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|`smc_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its strobes (`CHK-AXI-STRB`) and its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge over randomized address, data, wstrb, and size
* Stress of the 56-bit address / 64-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A byte image of every issued write's enabled lanes, compared at the end against every touched word of the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate, the lanes those words kept included
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random size, random size-aligned address over the full 56-bit address field with sub-beat offsets, random data, random legal wstrb) for the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
b. Snapshot the target word before each write and apply the write's enabled lanes to a byte image.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data and wstrb on the bus lanes the address selects, size, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Read every touched word back from the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate and compare it with the byte image: written lanes hold their last data, untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_axi_rand_req_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each write puts exactly one transaction on the port with the programmed address, size, strobes, and strobed data
|`smc_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image: written lanes hold their last data and untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-26-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SMC fabric bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x28) stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing: the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR both return the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`); no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable, not a one-shot power-on effect
* Buffered requests are dropped: with the first beat of a fixed-address series write held on the bus and up to the bridge's write pipeline depth plus one beats queued behind it, the disable lets the held beat complete, since an AXI master cannot withdraw a valid request, and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x28, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
b. Preload a RAM sentinel at the gated address, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28), and shift a full-strobe write request whose data differs from the sentinel.
c. Verify no AW/W transaction is issued on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, the sentinel is unchanged, and the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
d. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.
. Queued series beats (once):
a. Hold the responder's AW channel, with W not accepted ahead of AW, by a READY stall that outlasts the next two steps; program SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL for a fixed-address write with `pl_depth` at the bridge's write pipeline depth, shift the first beat and confirm its AW is on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, shift one to `pl_depth + 1` more beats, and confirm the SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY (every beat accepted).
b. Assert the disable; once the held beat completes, step TCK until the bridge state machine is idle, clear the stall and the disable, and step TCK again.
c. Verify exactly one write completed, no request activity followed it, and the slot holds the first beat's word; a legal write then completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI write appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay); the gated request carries all-ones strobes, so a leaked write overwrites it
|`smc_axi_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_axi_gate_queued_drop_chk` |The held beat completes and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release: exactly one write completes (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM), the request counters stay flat after it (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR` SV-UVM), and the slot keeps the first beat's word
|`smc_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI traffic, no memory mutation, and no TDR update, queued requests are dropped, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-27-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_test]]
===== 2.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single write

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op AXI4-Lite write through the SMC OTP bridge (SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x1C)
* 32-bit address / 32-bit data field formatting consistent with the SMC OTP CAPS TDR (IR=0x1B, AXI4-Lite, 32/32)
* Address/data/wstrb delivery to the AXI-Lite subordinate on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Op-status settles at OKAY (0b00) within the poll bound (`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`); the Busy (0b11) capture before completion is the stall scenarios' evidence (section 4). OKAY is the all-zero encoding, so the responder burst count (`CHK-AXI-NONVAC`) and the memory compare corroborate it

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load and verify the bridge CAPS:
a. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
b. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite, ADDR_SIZE = 32, DATA_SIZE = 32.
. Issue the single write:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in the 32-bit target address, 32-bit write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
. Poll for completion:
a. Re-enter Capture-DR/Shift-DR and read the op-status field until not Busy (0b11).
b. Verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the side effect:
a. Confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate received exactly one write, at the target address with the expected data and wstrb (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).
b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4-Lite, 32-bit address/data
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status settles at OKAY (0b00), corroborated by the responder burst count and the memory compare
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_data_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The AXI4-Lite single write completes with OKAY status and the SMC OTP subordinate captures the correct address and data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-27a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.7a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI-Lite single write delivers the expected 32-bit data word
* Read-back verifies byte-lane placement and final memory-visible value
* Write/read operations both return OKAY status

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SMC OTP AXI-Lite single write with a non-zero 32-bit pattern and valid write strobes.
. Poll until write status is OKAY and confirm the subordinate holds the pattern (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring word the same way, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Read the same SMC OTP address through the bridge.
. Compare the returned word against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written SMC OTP word
|`smc_otp_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SMC OTP written word is returned by bridge read-back with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-28-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SMC OTP bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) preloading the base address and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct AXI-Lite subordinate location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 32-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete, confirm the port carried it at base{plus}i*stride with the beat's data (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`), and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word there (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes, one per beat, and no transaction during the SERIES_CTRL scans (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each beat puts exactly one write on the port, at base {plus} i*stride over the full 32-bit address
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-29-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SMC OTP bridge all targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no-incr mode)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) holding one address while SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one AXI-Lite subordinate location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, each at the fixed address over the full address width (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).
b. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each of the N beats puts exactly one write on the port, at the fixed address over the full address width
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds each beat's word after that beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`) and the final (last-beat) data at the end
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-210-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4-Lite write series through the SMC OTP bridge carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.16>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its strobes (`CHK-AXI-STRB`) and its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-211-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.11 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SMC OTP bridge over randomized address, data, and wstrb
* Stress of the 32-bit address / 32-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A byte image of every issued write's enabled lanes, compared at the end against every touched word of the AXI-Lite subordinate, the lanes those words kept included
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random word-aligned address over the full 32-bit address field, random data, random legal wstrb) for the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
b. Snapshot the target word before each write and apply the write's enabled lanes to a byte image.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data, wstrb, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Read every touched word back from the AXI-Lite subordinate and compare it with the byte image: written lanes hold their last data, untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_otp_axi_rand_req_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each write puts exactly one transaction on the port with the programmed address, strobes, and strobed data
|`smc_otp_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image: written lanes hold their last data and untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-212-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SMC OTP bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x1C) stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing: the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR both return the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`); no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable
* Buffered requests are dropped: with the first beat of a fixed-address series write held on the bus and up to the bridge's write pipeline depth plus one beats queued behind it, the disable lets the held beat complete, since an AXI master cannot withdraw a valid request, and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x1C, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image). Assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, preload a RAM sentinel, load IR=0x1C, and shift a write request; verify no transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, the sentinel unchanged, and the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
b. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify normal operation resumes.
. Queued series beats (once):
a. Hold the responder's AW channel, with W not accepted ahead of AW, by a READY stall that outlasts the next two steps; program SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL for a fixed-address write with `pl_depth` at the bridge's write pipeline depth, shift the first beat and confirm its AW is on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, shift one to `pl_depth + 1` more beats, and confirm the SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY (every beat accepted).
b. Assert the disable; once the held beat completes, step TCK until the bridge state machine is idle, clear the stall and the disable, and step TCK again.
c. Verify exactly one write completed, no request activity followed it, and the slot holds the first beat's word.
. Final restore check:
a. With all disables clear, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_queued_drop_chk` |The held beat completes and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release: exactly one write completes (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM), the request counters stay flat after it (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR` SV-UVM), and the slot keeps the first beat's word
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI-Lite traffic, no memory mutation, and no TDR update, queued requests are dropped, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-213-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_test]]

* 32-bit address / 32-bit data field formatting consistent with the SEP OTP CAPS TDR (IR=0x21, AXI4-Lite, 32/32)

b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4-Lite, 32-bit address/data
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status settles at OKAY (0b00), corroborated by the responder burst count and the memory compare
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_data_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
