'''''

[[sec-213a-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.13a `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI-Lite single write delivers the expected 32-bit data word
* Read-back verifies byte-lane placement and final memory-visible value
* Write/read operations both return OKAY status

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SEP OTP AXI-Lite single write with a non-zero 32-bit pattern and valid write strobes.
. Poll until write status is OKAY and confirm the subordinate holds the pattern (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring word the same way, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Read the same SEP OTP address through the bridge.
. Compare the returned word against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written SEP OTP word
|`sep_otp_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SEP OTP written word is returned by bridge read-back with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-214-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) preloading the base address and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct AXI-Lite subordinate location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 32-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete, confirm the port carried it at base{plus}i*stride with the beat's data (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`), and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word there (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes, one per beat, and no transaction during the SERIES_CTRL scans (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each beat puts exactly one write on the port, at base {plus} i*stride over the full 32-bit address
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-215-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge all targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no-incr mode)
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) holding one address while SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one AXI-Lite subordinate location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm the port carried exactly N writes on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`, each at the fixed address over the full address width (`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`).
b. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each of the N beats puts exactly one write on the port, at the fixed address over the full address width
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds each beat's word after that beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`) and the final (last-beat) data at the end
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-216-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4-Lite write series through the SEP OTP bridge carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-425-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.25>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its strobes (`CHK-AXI-STRB`) and its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-217-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.17 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge over randomized address, data, and wstrb
* Stress of the 32-bit address / 32-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A byte image of every issued write's enabled lanes, compared at the end against every touched word of the AXI-Lite subordinate, the lanes those words kept included
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random word-aligned address over the full 32-bit address field, random data, random legal wstrb) for the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
b. Snapshot the target word before each write and apply the write's enabled lanes to a byte image.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data, wstrb, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Read every touched word back from the AXI-Lite subordinate and compare it with the byte image: written lanes hold their last data, untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_req_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each write puts exactly one transaction on the port with the programmed address, strobes, and strobed data
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image: written lanes hold their last data and untouched lanes their prior value (`CHK-J2A-MEM-IMAGE`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and every touched word of the subordinate matches the byte image, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-218-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SEP OTP bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x22) stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing: the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR both return the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`); no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable
* Buffered requests are dropped: with the first beat of a fixed-address series write held on the bus and up to the bridge's write pipeline depth plus one beats queued behind it, the disable lets the held beat complete, since an AXI master cannot withdraw a valid request, and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x22, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Before the disable, shift a seeded SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image). Assert `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, preload a RAM sentinel, load IR=0x22, and shift a write request; verify no transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`, the sentinel unchanged, and the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
b. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify normal operation resumes.
. Queued series beats (once):
a. Hold the responder's AW channel, with W not accepted ahead of AW, by a READY stall that outlasts the next two steps; program SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL for a fixed-address write with `pl_depth` at the bridge's write pipeline depth, shift the first beat and confirm its AW is on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`, shift one to `pl_depth + 1` more beats, and confirm the SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY (every beat accepted).
b. Assert the disable; once the held beat completes, step TCK until the bridge state machine is idle, clear the stall and the disable, and step TCK again.
c. Verify exactly one write completed, no request activity followed it, and the slot holds the first beat's word.
. Final restore check:
a. With all disables clear, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_queued_drop_chk` |The held beat completes and no queued beat reaches the bus while gated or after the release: exactly one write completes (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM), the request counters stay flat after it (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AW`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-W`, `CHK-AXI-GATE-AR` SV-UVM), and the slot keeps the first beat's word
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI-Lite traffic, no memory mutation, and no TDR update, queued requests are dropped, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios]]
==== 3. JTAG2AXI Read Scenarios

[[sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric AXI4 single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) DR field packing: address (56-bit), data, wstrb, size, op
* Read op encoding `op=0b01` with data=0 and wstrb=0, and busy/terminal status polling
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4, addr_size = 56, data_size = 64.
. Issue a single write through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge to a chosen target address.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, size, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring beat through the same bridge and verify it, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Read back the same address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Reload SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11), then read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the previously written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both settle at op=0b00 (OKAY) within the poll bound
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` model
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at the target address matches the issued write (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4 accesses through the SMC fabric bridge across a block of incrementing addresses
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) preloading the start address and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test,2.5>> and <<sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.5>>; every SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) shift carries its own address

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 64-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write to complete and confirm `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback after the write and read series holds the address base{plus}N*stride (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reports OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_axi_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_axi_series_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content over the block matches the issued write series, after each beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test]]
===== 3.2a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric 32-bit incrementing series write-read at beat offset +4

*Test Coverage*:

* A SERIES_DATA_INCR write then read stream with `size=2` whose base is beat-aligned plus four bytes
* Write-side AXI lane placement of the 32-bit logical payload onto the high half of the 64-bit beat
* Capture-DR returning that same logical payload extracted from the address-selected AXI RDATA lanes
* Per-beat memory compare at `base+i*4` against the written 32-bit pattern

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base with seeded address bits above the responder window, add four, set stride to four bytes, choose a beat count N, and preload every 64-bit beat the stream touches with a seeded word; in a seeded half of the passes the stream crosses the window top, carrying the series address into those bits.
. Write the series through SERIES_CTRL(WRITE) + SERIES_DATA_INCR; after each beat, confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location at `base+i*4` holds the 32-bit payload and the SERIES_CTRL capture reports OKAY at `base+(i+1)*4`.
. Read the series back in the same order through SERIES_CTRL(READ) + SERIES_DATA_INCR; Capture-DR returns the logical 32-bit payload, and the SERIES_CTRL capture after each read reports OKAY at the advanced address.
. Compare every returned beat with the written pattern and the responder memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_status_chk` |The SERIES_CTRL capture after every write beat and every read holds the advanced address (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reports OKAY
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_rdata_chk` |Each Capture-DR beat matches the written 32-bit payload
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at each size-aligned address matches the issued write (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every 32-bit incrementing write and read completes OKAY, Capture-DR returns the logical payload from the address-selected lanes, and all per-beat comparisons match, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-33-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4 writes and reads through the SMC fabric bridge targeting one fixed address (no address advance)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) holding one address while SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) carries every beat without advancing it
* Repeated reads of the fixed address: the first returns the last word written through the bridge, and before each later read the subordinate slot takes a fresh seeded word, so every capture is of its own read
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write to complete and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds that word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: from the second iteration on, first write a fresh seeded word to the target address through the responder backdoor; then load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify the first returned beat equals the last word written and each later beat the word placed before its read.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |The first returned beat equals the last word written; each later beat returns the word placed before its read
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` at the fixed address holds each word written, after its beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the word its read found, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-34-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4 write then read series through the SMC fabric bridge carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches, and a held-address re-read returns the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.7>> and <<sec-48-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.8>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`); shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`smc_axi_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written once the first read of it completes
|`smc_axi_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_axi_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC fabric AXI4 read addresses and sizes against a randomized reference
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) field packing under randomized address/size stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate a seeded sequence of reads: one at each of the four transfer sizes, then reads with a random legal size; each address is a random size-aligned address over the full 56-bit address field, sub-beat offsets included.
. Before each read, preload the whole 64-bit beat that holds its address in `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` with a seeded word.
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare the data-field bytes on the lanes each address selects with the preloaded bytes.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read settles at op=0b00 (OKAY) within the poll bound
|`smc_axi_random_rdata_chk` |The data-field bytes on the lanes each address selects match the preloaded bytes
|`smc_axi_random_axsize_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each read puts exactly one transaction on the port, with ARADDR and ARSIZE equal to the programmed address and size
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` gates the SMC fabric read bridge
* Gated read requests are suppressed: the SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`), no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, and the request-activity counters hold flat across a blocked window that stays open through the disable's release (no delayed replay)
* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read returning OKAY with expected data from `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`.
. Gate (per pass, twice): before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28), and shift a read request.
a. Snapshot the AW/W/AR activity counters before the attempt and verify no AXI transaction issues on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` across the gated window, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset; hold the blocked window across the release and verify no queued request replays, then verify a normal read is restored with an exact activity delta (only the sanctioned restore read).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_rd_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |The blocked window stays clean across the disable's release
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the bit clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable suppresses all AXI activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test]]
===== 3.6a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating with explicit no-AXI-activity check

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` prevents any SMC fabric read request from reaching AXI (deriving it from the lifecycle enables is SEP-level, <<dtp-vplan-scope>>)
* No `AWVALID`, `WVALID`, or `ARVALID` activity occurs while the bridge is gated on both passes or across the release, and the gated SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR stays in the scan path and latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`)
* Normal read activity resumes after the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read and confirm AXI activity is observed.
. Before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, and attempt a SMC fabric read; repeat the gate on a second pass.
. Monitor AXI request channels for a bounded window and verify no AW/W/AR valid activity occurs, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset, verify no request replays across the release, and verify a normal read again produces exactly its own AXI activity and OKAY status.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_no_aw_ar_chk` |No AWVALID, WVALID, or ARVALID while the bridge is gated or across its release
|`smc_rd_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_activity_chk` |AXI read activity resumes after gating clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable blocks all SMC fabric AXI read activity on both passes and normal reads resume after each release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-37-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring beat through the same bridge and verify it, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Read back the same address through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both settle at op=0b00 (OKAY) within the poll bound
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4-Lite accesses through the SMC OTP bridge across a block of incrementing 32-bit addresses
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) preloading the start address and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-211-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test,2.11>> and <<sec-311-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.11>>; every SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) shift carries its own address

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback after the write and read series holds the address base{plus}N*stride (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reports OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_otp_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_otp_series_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content over the block matches the issued write series, after each beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_oversize_test]]
===== 3.8a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_oversize_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write-read with a size field wider than the bus

*Test Coverage*:

* The effective transfer size on a 32-bit bridge: the 2-bit size field at its maximum, 3 (8 bytes), selects one full 32-bit beat
* Full 32-bit writes and reads on every beat, compared by the shared AXI scoreboard
* A SERIES_DATA_INCR register of 32 payload bits and a series address that advances by 4 bytes per beat
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride of 4 bytes, and a beat count N; draw a distinct 32-bit data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size=3, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through the 32-bit SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) register; Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*4 and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake and compare the subordinate word at base{plus}i*4 with the data written.
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size=3, and address base{plus}i*4; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*4.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_incr_oversize_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at base{plus}i*4 matches each issued write, so no beat lands at the 8-byte stride
|`smc_otp_series_incr_oversize_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_otp_series_incr_oversize_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and a final address one 4-byte stride past the last beat
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every beat is written to and read from its 4-byte-stride address, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the expected final address, and no AXI protocol or scoreboard/reference-model errors occur.

'''''

[[sec-39-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4-Lite writes and reads through the SMC OTP bridge targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no address advance)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) holding one address while SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) carries every beat without advancing it
* Repeated reads of the fixed address: the first returns the last word written through the bridge, and before each later read the subordinate slot takes a fresh seeded word, so every capture is of its own read
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds that word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: from the second iteration on, first write a fresh seeded word to the target address through the responder backdoor; then load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify the first returned beat equals the last word written and each later beat the word placed before its read.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |The first returned beat equals the last word written; each later beat returns the word placed before its read
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate at the fixed address holds each word written, after its beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the word its read found, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-310-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4-Lite write then read series through the SMC OTP bridge carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches, and a held-address re-read returns the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.16>> and <<sec-417-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.17>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`); shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes.
d. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`smc_otp_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written once the first read of it completes
|`smc_otp_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`smc_otp_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-311-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.11 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC OTP AXI4-Lite read addresses against a randomized 32-bit reference
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) field packing under randomized address stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate a seeded sequence of word-aligned read addresses over the full 32-bit address field.
. Before each read, preload the word at its address in the SMC OTP AXI-Lite subordinate with a seeded value.
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the AXI-Lite subordinate reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read settles at op=0b00 (OKAY) within the poll bound
|`smc_otp_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized AXI-Lite reference
|`smc_otp_random_req_chk` |`CHK-J2A-BUS-REQ`: each read puts exactly one transaction on the port at the programmed address
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-312-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1` gates the SMC OTP read bridge
* Gated read requests are suppressed: the SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`), and no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, with no delayed replay after the disable is released
* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC OTP read returning OKAY with expected data from the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Gate (per pass, twice): before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C), and shift a read request.
a. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction issues on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` across the gated window, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify no queued request replays, then verify normal reads resume.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI-Lite transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |No queued request replays after the disable's release
|`smc_otp_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the disable clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi` suppresses all AXI-Lite activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-313-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.13 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SEP_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x21) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Write a second, distinct word to the neighbouring beat through the same bridge and verify it, so the SINGLE_OP data field holds a word other than the one the read must return.
. Read back the same address through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both settle at op=0b00 (OKAY) within the poll bound
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`sep_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-314-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4-Lite accesses through the SEP OTP bridge across a block of incrementing 32-bit addresses
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) preloading the start address and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-217-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test,2.17>> and <<sec-317-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.17>>; every SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) shift carries its own address

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat count N, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat-aligned base address with seeded address bits above the responder window, whose N beats cross the window top in a seeded half of the passes, carrying the series address into those bits; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback after the write and read series holds the address base{plus}N*stride (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reports OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`sep_otp_series_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content over the block matches the issued write series, after each beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-315-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4-Lite writes and reads through the SEP OTP bridge targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no address advance)
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) holding one address while SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25) carries every beat without advancing it
* Repeated reads of the fixed address: the first returns the last word written through the bridge, and before each later read the subordinate slot takes a fresh seeded word, so every capture is of its own read
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address with seeded bits above the responder window and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write to complete and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds that word at the target address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`).
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: from the second iteration on, first write a fresh seeded word to the target address through the responder backdoor; then load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify the first returned beat equals the last word written and each later beat the word placed before its read.
. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`sep_otp_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |The first returned beat equals the last word written; each later beat returns the word placed before its read
|`sep_otp_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate at the fixed address holds each word written, after its beat (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the word its read found, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-316-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4-Lite write then read series through the SEP OTP bridge carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches, and a held-address re-read returns the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-425-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.25>> and <<sec-426-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.26>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits one responder window, with seeded address bits above the window, and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`); shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written to the slot once the first read of it completes.
d. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`sep_otp_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the word its address holds when the read launches: the last word written, or for the held-address re-read the fresh word written once the first read of it completes
|`sep_otp_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat does not advance the address)
|`sep_otp_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-317-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test]]

. Issue each randomized read through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-318-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_read_security_gating_test]]

* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

a. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction issues on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` across the gated window, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.

|`sep_otp_rd_gate_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`sep_otp_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |No queued request replays after the disable's release

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi` suppresses all AXI-Lite activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-4-jtag2axi-error-backpressure-cdc-and-decode-scenarios]]
==== 4. JTAG2AXI Error, Backpressure, CDC, and Decode Scenarios

[[sec-41-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC fabric AXI4 debug bridge (SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating region
* AXI4 subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (BRESP = SLVERR or DECERR) captured in op[1:0] (0b10 / 0b01)
* No spurious or partial write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`
* Bridge does not hang or corrupt internal state; a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 subordinate (OcahAxiSlaveAgent) to return SLVERR or DECERR on writes to the chosen error region.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x28):
a. Shift in the error-region target address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, size, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status field via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (op != 0b11).
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR) and not OKAY (0b00).
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the AXI subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data/wstrb at the subordinate, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`smc_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption) and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious memory update occurs, and the bridge recovers for the next legal transaction, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-42-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_read_test]]
===== 4.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_single_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op read through the SMC fabric AXI4 debug bridge (IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating region
* AXI4 subordinate returns a non-OKAY read response (RRESP = SLVERR or DECERR) captured in op[1:0] (0b01 / 0b10)
* The read-data field returns the RDATA of the errored beat: the bridge latches the beat's data with its status, so the capture equals the word the subordinate drove on that beat and not the value preloaded in the slot
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload a nonzero word in the error slot and configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4 subordinate to return SLVERR or DECERR on reads to the error region; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the preload and from every word an earlier read of the pass returned.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x28):
a. Shift in the error-region target address, data = 0, wstrb = 0, size, op = 0b01 (Read).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 read on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code and that the read-data field equals the seeded word, which the passive monitor observed on the errored beat, differing from the preloaded slot value.
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference value; verify OKAY and matching read data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_rdata_chk` |`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`: returned read data equals the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preloaded slot value
|`smc_axi_err_single_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY (`CHK-AXI-RDATA`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the read-data field carries the errored beat's RDATA, and the bridge recovers for the next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-43-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric no-increment write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat; address mode remains fixed for every beat
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat while good beats commit correctly to the fixed address
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access, which completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat series at the fixed target address, armed after the first beat commits.
. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); every Update-DR issues a write at the fixed address.
. After each beat's AXI write handshake, check the memory at the fixed address; after the good beat before the fault and after the faulting beat, capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, good beats committed their data to the fixed address, the faulting beat left the memory unchanged, and the AXI address never incremented on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
