|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit to the fixed address; no address increment observed
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The injected fault is reported on the failing beat, the address stays fixed for all beats, good beats commit correctly, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-44-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric no-increment read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat while the AXI address remains fixed
* Good beats return the reference value at the fixed address; the failing beat's capture is the errored beat's RDATA, not the preload
* SERIES_CTRL status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with a known value at the fixed address and a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat read series, armed after the first beat's read completes; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the known value.
. For each beat: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the fixed-address reference; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`smc_axi_err_noincr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the fixed-address reference, the failing beat reports the injected error, the address stays fixed, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-45-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A)
* One beat in the auto-incrementing sequence hits an error region and returns SLVERR/DECERR
* Good beats before/after the fault commit to their correctly-incremented addresses
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); every Update-DR issues a write and advances the series address by the stride.
. After each beat's AXI write handshake, check the memory at the beat's address; after each beat up to the faulting one, capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds that beat's address plus one stride, and its status reads OKAY after each good beat and carries the injected code right after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, and the address progression on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: each capture holds the beat address plus one stride
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats commit at the expected incrementing addresses, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-46-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A)
* One beat of the stream returns SLVERR/DECERR mid-series; SERIES_CTRL is programmed with each beat's absolute address, since the bridge admits `pipeline_depth+1` plain read pushes per programming, and every beat's SERIES_CTRL capture proves the auto-increment
* Good beats return reference data at their addresses
* The SERIES_CTRL capture after every beat holds the beat address plus one stride, and after the failing beat its status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with reference data across the address range and configure a non-OKAY response at one address; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from every preload.
. For each beat i: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field must equal base{plus}i*stride{plus}stride, and its status field must read OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carry the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data at their addresses, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and every capture's address matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: the SERIES_CTRL capture after each beat holds that beat's address plus one stride (the launched read advances the address, errored or not)
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the reference data at their addresses; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the expected references, every SERIES_CTRL capture shows the incremented address, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test]]
===== 4.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing write with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), with a fault mid-series
* Each with-error-status shift returns the previous beat's error status, so the fault surfaces on the shift after the faulting beat without a SERIES_CTRL capture per beat
* A trailing shift returns the last beat's status; SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Good beats commit at their incremented addresses and the faulting beat leaves its address unchanged; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat with increment 1 through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C) and run the series to completion; each Capture-DR returns the status bit of the previous beat.
. After the last beat, shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) to return the last beat's status bit; after the faulting beat, capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and read its address field, the faulting beat's address plus one stride, and its status field.
. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting beat, good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the faulting beat and 0 on every other shift; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code; `CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: its address is the faulting beat's address plus one stride
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_axi_err_incr_wr_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing beat and error code, good beats commit, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-48-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test]]
===== 4.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing read with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge, carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), with a fault mid-series
* One SERIES_CTRL preload serves the whole read stream: the bridge advances the address per launching shift, and each shift returns the data and error status of the previous read
* Good beats return reference data with status bit 0; the shift returning the faulting read carries status bit 1 and SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate with reference data and configure a non-OKAY response at one address in the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift N words with increment 1 followed by one trailing word with increment 0 through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); shift k returns the data and status bit of read k-1.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL right after the faulting read's handshake and read its status field.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented addresses, the status bit is 1 only for the faulting read, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 only on the shift returning the faulting read; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_axi_err_incr_rd_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-49-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_security_gating_test]]
===== 4.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric bridge gating: `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`
* When gated, SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) requests are dropped (DTP JTAG document, \"Debug Disable\"): the TDR stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`), no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, even on error-path attempts, so no response reaches the error status, and none replays after the release
* Two assert/release passes prove the gate is repeatable; each release restores normal and error-path operation

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); with the subordinate configured to return errors on the target region, assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` to gate the bridge.
. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) and attempt an error-region write:
a. Verify no AXI transaction is issued on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated or after the release, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify a normal (and error-path) JTAG2AXI access resumes and the error is reported as expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 as a second pass of the same disable.
. Confirm that with all disables clear, ungated error-path accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_err_gating_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI traffic on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_axi_err_gating_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_axi_err_gating_restore_chk` |Each release restores normal/error-path operation; the ungated error of each pass, SLVERR in one and DECERR in the other, reports its code (`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`); from the snapshot before the gated attempt to the end of the pass the bridge carries exactly the ungated error write and the recovery write (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable suppresses all AXI traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-410-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge (SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x1C) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate (AXI-Lite wrapper) to return SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x1C):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption) and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-411-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_read_test]]
===== 4.11 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_single_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op read through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x1C) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY read response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* The read-data field returns the RDATA of the errored beat: the bridge latches the beat's data with its status, so the capture equals the word the subordinate drove on that beat and not the value preloaded in the slot
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload a nonzero word in the error slot and configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return SLVERR/DECERR on reads to the error region; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the preload and from every word an earlier read of the pass returned.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x1C):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, data = 0, wstrb = 0, op = 0b01 (Read).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite read on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code and the read-data field equals the seeded word, which the passive monitor observed on the errored beat, differing from the preloaded slot value.
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference value; verify OKAY and matching data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_rdata_chk` |`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`: returned read data equals the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preloaded slot value
|`smc_otp_axi_err_single_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY (`CHK-AXI-RDATA`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the read-data field carries the errored beat's RDATA, and the bridge recovers for the next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-412-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E)
* One beat in the auto-incrementing sequence returns SLVERR/DECERR
* Good beats before/after the fault commit to their correctly-incremented addresses
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); every Update-DR issues a write and advances the series address by the stride.
. After each beat's AXI-Lite write handshake, check the memory at the beat's address; after each beat up to the faulting one, capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds that beat's address plus one stride, and its status reads OKAY after each good beat and carries the injected code right after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, and the address progression on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: each capture holds the beat address plus one stride
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats commit at the expected incrementing addresses, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-413-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.13 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E)
* One address in the auto-incrementing range returns SLVERR/DECERR mid-series
* Good beats before the fault return reference data at their incremented addresses
* SERIES_CTRL status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with reference data across the range and configure a non-OKAY response at one address; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from every preload.
. For each beat i: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field must equal base{plus}i*stride{plus}stride, and its status field must read OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carry the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data at their incremented addresses, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and the address progression matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: the SERIES_CTRL capture after each beat holds that beat's address plus one stride (the launched read advances the address, errored or not)
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the expected incrementing-address references, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-414-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP no-increment write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat; AXI-Lite address remains fixed for every beat
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat while good beats commit correctly to the fixed address
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat series at the fixed target address, armed after the first beat commits.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); every Update-DR issues a write at the fixed address.
. After each beat's AXI-Lite write handshake, check the memory at the fixed address; after the good beat before the fault and after the faulting beat, capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, good beats committed their data to the fixed address, the faulting beat left the memory unchanged, and the AXI-Lite address never incremented on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit to the fixed address; no address increment observed
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The injected fault is reported on the failing beat, the address stays fixed, good beats commit correctly, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-415-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP no-increment read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) read series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat while the AXI-Lite address remains fixed
* Good beats return the reference value at the fixed address; the failing beat's capture is the errored beat's RDATA, not the preload
* SERIES_CTRL status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with a known value at the fixed address and a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat read series, armed after the first beat's read completes; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the known value.
. For each beat: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the fixed-address reference; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`smc_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the fixed-address reference, the failing beat reports the injected error, the address stays fixed, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test]]
===== 4.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), with a fault mid-series
* Each with-error-status shift returns the previous beat's error status, so the fault surfaces on the shift after the faulting beat without a SERIES_CTRL capture per beat
* A trailing shift returns the last beat's status; SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Good beats commit at their incremented addresses and the faulting beat leaves its address unchanged; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat with increment 1 through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20) and run the series to completion; each Capture-DR returns the status bit of the previous beat.
. After the last beat, shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) to return the last beat's status bit; after the faulting beat, capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and read its address field, the faulting beat's address plus one stride, and its status field.
. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting beat, good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the faulting beat and 0 on every other shift; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code; `CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: its address is the faulting beat's address plus one stride
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing beat and error code, good beats commit, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-417-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test]]
===== 4.17 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP read with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), with a fault mid-series
* One SERIES_CTRL preload serves the whole read stream: the bridge advances the address per launching shift, and each shift returns the data and error status of the previous read
* Good beats return reference data with status bit 0; the shift returning the faulting read carries status bit 1 and SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with reference data and configure a non-OKAY response at one address in the incrementing range.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift N words with increment 1 followed by one trailing word with increment 0 through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20); shift k returns the data and status bit of read k-1.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL right after the faulting read's handshake and read its status field.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented addresses, the status bit is 1 only for the faulting read, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 only on the shift returning the faulting read; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`smc_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-418-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test]]
===== 4.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP bridge gating: `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1`
* When gated, SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) requests are dropped (DTP JTAG document, \"Debug Disable\"): the TDR stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`), no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`, even on error-path attempts, so no response reaches the error status, and none replays after the release
* Two assert/release passes prove the gate is repeatable; each release restores normal and error-path operation

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Before the disable, shift a seeded SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); with the AXI-Lite subordinate configured to return errors on the target region, assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1` to gate the bridge.
. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) and attempt an error-region write:
a. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction is issued on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated or after the release, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify normal/error-path operation resumes and the error is reported as expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 as a second pass of the same disable.
. Confirm that with all disables clear, ungated error-path accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`smc_otp_axi_err_gating_restore_chk` |Each release restores normal/error-path operation; the ungated error of each pass, SLVERR in one and DECERR in the other, reports its code (`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`); from the snapshot before the gated attempt to the end of the pass the bridge carries exactly the ungated error write and the recovery write (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable suppresses all AXI-Lite traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-419-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_write_test]]
===== 4.19 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge (SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x22) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x22):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_mem_chk` |No unintended write side effect at the error-region address
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption) and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-420-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_read_test]]
===== 4.20 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_single_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op read through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge (IR=0x22) targeting an error region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY read response (SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op[1:0]
* The read-data field returns the RDATA of the errored beat: the bridge latches the beat's data with its status, so the capture equals the word the subordinate drove on that beat and not the value preloaded in the slot
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload a nonzero word in the error slot and configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return SLVERR/DECERR on reads to the error region; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the preload and from every word an earlier read of the pass returned.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` (IR=0x22):
a. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, data = 0, wstrb = 0, op = 0b01 (Read).
b. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite read on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Poll the op-status until not Busy.
. Verify op[1:0] reflects the injected error code and the read-data field equals the seeded word, which the passive monitor observed on the errored beat, differing from the preloaded slot value.
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference value; verify OKAY and matching data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_rd_rdata_chk` |`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`: returned read data equals the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preloaded slot value
|`sep_otp_axi_err_single_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY (`CHK-AXI-RDATA`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the read-data field carries the errored beat's RDATA, and the bridge recovers for the next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-421-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.21 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24)
* One beat in the auto-incrementing sequence returns SLVERR/DECERR
* Good beats before/after the fault commit to their correctly-incremented addresses
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24); every Update-DR issues a write and advances the series address by the stride.
. After each beat's AXI-Lite write handshake, check the memory at the beat's address; after each beat up to the faulting one, capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds that beat's address plus one stride, and its status reads OKAY after each good beat and carries the injected code right after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, and the address progression on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: each capture holds the beat address plus one stride
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats commit at the expected incrementing addresses, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-422-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.22 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24)
* One address in the auto-incrementing range returns SLVERR/DECERR mid-series
* Good beats before the fault return reference data at their incremented addresses
* SERIES_CTRL status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with reference data across the range and configure a non-OKAY response at one address; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from every preload.
. For each beat i: load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
. Shift SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field must equal base{plus}i*stride{plus}stride, and its status field must read OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carry the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data at their incremented addresses, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and the address progression matches the expected increment.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: the SERIES_CTRL capture after each beat holds that beat's address plus one stride (the launched read advances the address, errored or not)
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the expected incrementing-address references, the failing beat reports the injected error, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-423-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test]]
===== 4.23 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_write_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP no-increment write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) write series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat; AXI-Lite address remains fixed for every beat
* SERIES_CTRL status identifies the failing beat while good beats commit correctly to the fixed address
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat series at the fixed target address, armed after the first beat commits.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25); every Update-DR issues a write at the fixed address.
. After each beat's AXI-Lite write handshake, check the memory at the fixed address; after the good beat before the fault and after the faulting beat, capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code, good beats committed their data to the fixed address, the faulting beat left the memory unchanged, and the AXI-Lite address never incremented on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing beat
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_mem_chk` |Good beats commit to the fixed address; no address increment observed
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_wr_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The injected fault is reported on the failing beat, the address stays fixed, good beats commit correctly, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-424-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test]]
===== 4.24 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_no_incr_read_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP no-increment read error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI fixed-address (no-incr) read series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25)
* Mid-series SLVERR/DECERR injected on one beat while the AXI-Lite address remains fixed
* Good beats return the reference value at the fixed address; the failing beat's capture is the errored beat's RDATA, not the preload
* SERIES_CTRL status matches the injected fault; bridge recovers afterward

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with a known value at the fixed address and a non-OKAY response on the middle beat of a three-beat read series, armed after the first beat's read completes; the errored beat carries a seeded nonzero word that differs from the known value.
. For each beat: load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the fixed address; Update-DR.
. Shift SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25) once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again to capture the returned beat.
. After every beat, capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL: its address field holds the fixed address, and its status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected code after the faulting beat.
. Verify good beats return the reference data, the SERIES_CTRL status after the failing beat carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code with that beat's capture equal to the seeded errored-beat word rather than the preload, and the address never incremented.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_status_chk` |`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status reads OKAY after the good beat before the fault and carries the injected error code right after the failing read beat
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_addr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: every SERIES_CTRL capture holds the fixed address
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the fixed-address reference; the failing beat's capture is the seeded word the errored beat carried and differs from the preload (`CHK-J2A-ERR-RDATA`)
|`sep_otp_axi_err_noincr_rd_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Good beats match the fixed-address reference, the failing beat reports the injected error, the address stays fixed, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-425-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test]]
===== 4.25 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address write series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26), with a fault mid-series
* Each with-error-status shift returns the previous beat's error status, so the fault surfaces on the shift after the faulting beat without a SERIES_CTRL capture per beat
* A trailing shift returns the last beat's status; SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Good beats commit at their incremented addresses and the faulting beat leaves its address unchanged; bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return a non-OKAY response at one address within the incrementing range.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift each beat with increment 1 through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26) and run the series to completion; each Capture-DR returns the status bit of the previous beat.
. After the last beat, shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) to return the last beat's status bit; after the faulting beat, capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and read its address field, the faulting beat's address plus one stride, and its status field.
. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting beat, good beats committed at their incremented addresses, the faulting beat left its address unchanged, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) write to a clean address; verify OKAY, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the faulting beat and 0 on every other shift; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code; `CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`: its address is the faulting beat's address plus one stride
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_mem_chk` |Good beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_wr_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal write completes with OKAY and lands its bytes (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing beat and error code, good beats commit, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-426-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test]]
===== 4.26 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP read with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite bridge, carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26), with a fault mid-series
* One SERIES_CTRL preload serves the whole read stream: the bridge advances the address per launching shift, and each shift returns the data and error status of the previous read
* Good beats return reference data with status bit 0; the shift returning the faulting read carries status bit 1 and SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
* Bridge recovers for a subsequent legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah_axi_vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate with reference data and configure a non-OKAY response at one address in the incrementing range.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
. Shift N words with increment 1 followed by one trailing word with increment 0 through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26); shift k returns the data and status bit of read k-1.
. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL right after the faulting read's handshake and read its status field.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented addresses, the status bit is 1 only for the faulting read, and the SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Issue a follow-up legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) read; verify OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 only on the shift returning the faulting read; `CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`: SERIES_CTRL status carries the injected SLVERR/DECERR code
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_rdata_chk` |Good beats match the incremented-address reference data
|`sep_otp_axi_err_incr_rd_status_recover_chk` |A subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The with-error-status bit and the SERIES_CTRL status report the failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, and the bridge recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-427-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test]]
===== 4.27 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_security_gating_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP bridge gating: `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1`
* When gated, SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) requests are dropped (DTP JTAG document, \"Debug Disable\"): the TDR stays in the scan path and its Update-DR latches nothing (`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`), no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`, even on error-path attempts, so no response reaches the error status, and none replays after the release
* Two assert/release passes prove the gate is repeatable; each release restores normal and error-path operation

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Before the disable, shift a seeded SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP NOP image twice, its size, strobe and data nonzero and its address unlike the gated request's, and keep the second capture (the reference image); with the AXI-Lite subordinate configured to return errors on the target region, assert `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1` to gate the bridge.
. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) and attempt an error-region write:
a. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction is issued on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated or after the release, and that the gated request's capture and a NOP capture after it equal the reference image.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify normal/error-path operation resumes and the error is reported as expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 as a second pass of the same disable.
. Confirm that with all disables clear, ungated error-path accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_err_gating_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite traffic on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_axi_err_gating_tdr_chk` |`CHK-J2A-GATE-TDR`: with the disable asserted, the gated request's Capture-DR and a NOP capture after its Update-DR equal the SINGLE_OP image captured before the disable in the op, size, wstrb, data, and address fields, and the request differs from that image in a latched field
|`sep_otp_axi_err_gating_restore_chk` |Each release restores normal/error-path operation; the ungated error of each pass, SLVERR in one and DECERR in the other, reports its code (`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`); from the snapshot before the gated attempt to the end of the pass the bridge carries exactly the ungated error write and the recovery write (`CHK-AXI-NOACT` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-GATE-EXACT` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable issues no AXI-Lite traffic on the error path, none replays after the release, and operation is fully restored after each release, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-428-dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_aw_before_w_test]]
===== 4.28 `dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_aw_before_w_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: AW before W channel skew

*Test Coverage*:

* Maximum AW/W channel skew on the JTAG2AXI single-op write path with AW ready asserted while W ready is held low
* The bridge port's `ocah_axi_sva` rules `OCAH_AXI_W_VALID_HELD` and `OCAH_AXI_W_PAYLOAD_STABLE` evaluated while W ready is low: the port's `tb_top` stall counters count the W stall and no AW stall cycle (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`)
* The bench does not sample the FSM state sequence: it observes the bridge on the write path under the stall, and judges the skewed write by the port's stability rules, its stall counters, and its OKAY completion

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive a JTAG2AXI single-op write through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`:
a. Program the bridge address and data via the primary TAP and launch the write op.
b. Configure the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate to skew the channels: accept AW (ready high) while holding W ready low for an extended interval.
. While W ready is low, confirm W valid and payload remain stable and do not deassert; across the write the port's W stall counter advances and its AW stall counter does not (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`).
. While W ready is held (a stall sized to outlast the first status poll), observe the stall from the bridge's side: its AXI state machine sits on the write path (`CHK-J2A-STALL-FSM`, through the `dtp_tb_if` state flags) and the first SINGLE_OP status capture reads Busy (`CHK-J2A-STALL-BUSY`).
. Release the W-channel backpressure and let the write drain.
. Verify the write completes with an OKAY response and read back the written location to confirm the value landed.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_bp_stall_chk` |While W ready is held the bridge FSM is observed on the write path and the first status poll reads Busy (`CHK-J2A-STALL-FSM`, `CHK-J2A-STALL-BUSY`)
|`j2a_bp_stable_chk` |W valid and payload remain stable while the subordinate holds W ready low; the port's `tb_top` stall counters count the W stall and no AW stall cycle (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`)
|`j2a_bp_status_chk` |Final JTAG2AXI completion status is OKAY and read-back data matches the written value (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The maximum-skew write holds the `ocah_axi_sva` W stability rules across the counted W stall, its AW is accepted with no stall cycle, and it completes correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-429-dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_long_stall_test]]
===== 4.29 `dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_long_stall_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Long ready stalls

*Test Coverage*:

* Long subordinate READY stalls on every bridge: AW and W held together on a write, AW held alone on a second write, AR on a read (4.28 holds W alone)
* The bridge port's `ocah_axi_sva` rules `OCAH_AXI_<CH>_VALID_HELD` and `OCAH_AXI_<CH>_PAYLOAD_STABLE` for AW, W, and AR stay silent across stall windows the bridge is observed inside, and the port's `tb_top` stall counter of each stalled channel counts the stall (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`)
* The JTAG2AXI state machine observed on the write path and on the read path under a prolonged stall (the `dtp_tb_if` path flags)
* No premature timeout, abort, or status corruption while a legal stall is in progress
* The settle point of each stalled operation: the AW+W write outlasts four status polls (the long-wait poll class), the AW-only write and the read settle within three (the short-wait class)
* A write with no READY stall whose status is captured only after the bridge drops its pending flag reads OKAY on its first capture

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set up stall stimulus on the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate:
a. Configure long, deterministic READY-low intervals on AW and W together and on AW alone (write path), and on AR (read path), that remain inside the architectural stall budget.
. Issue write traffic through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`:
a. Launch a single-op write while AW/W ready are stalled for longer than the first status poll; observe valid/payload hold stable across the full stall window with the port's AW and W stall counters advancing (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`), the bridge FSM on the write path, and the first status capture reading Busy (`CHK-J2A-STALL-FSM`, `CHK-J2A-STALL-BUSY`).
b. Release the stall and confirm OKAY completion with the written word in memory.
c. Launch a single-op write while only AW ready is stalled for longer than the first status poll; observe the AW stall counter advance (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD` on AW), the bridge FSM on the write path, and the first status capture reading Busy, then confirm OKAY completion with the written word in memory. W is not judged: the responder accepts the W beat while AW waits, which the SV-UVM responder does with its W-before-AW order armed for this write.
. Issue read traffic through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`:
a. Launch a single-op read while AR ready is stalled for longer than the first status poll; observe AR valid/address hold stable with the port's AR stall counter advancing (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`), the bridge FSM on the read path, and the first status capture reading Busy.
b. Release the stall and confirm the returned data matches the reference model.
. Issue a single-op write with no READY stall, step TCK in Run-Test/Idle until the bridge's pending flag clears, then capture SINGLE_OP and confirm OKAY with the written word in memory.
. Verify the bridge does not declare a timeout or error during any legal stall and that each operation reaches its expected terminal status after release.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_long_stall_assert_chk` |The port's `ocah_axi_sva` `OCAH_AXI_<CH>_VALID_HELD` and `OCAH_AXI_<CH>_PAYLOAD_STABLE` rules for AW, W, and AR stay silent across the full stall window, and the stall counter of every stalled channel advanced across the op (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`)
|`j2a_long_stall_status_chk` |While each stall holds the bridge FSM is on the stalled path and the first status poll reads Busy (`CHK-J2A-STALL-FSM`, `CHK-J2A-STALL-BUSY`); no premature timeout/abort; each op reaches its expected terminal status after stall release
|`j2a_long_stall_data_chk` |Post-stall write-back and read-data compare against the reference model (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: No AW/W/AR stability assertion fires under long stalls, every stalled channel's stall counter advanced (`CHK-J2A-STALL-HOLD`), every stall is observed from the bridge's side (`CHK-J2A-STALL-FSM`, `CHK-J2A-STALL-BUSY` on all three bridges), no operation times out or aborts during a legal stall, and every read/write completes correctly after release with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-430-dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_abort_at_data_w_test]]
===== 4.30 `dtp_jtag2axi_backpressure_abort_at_data_w_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Reset abort around data phase

*Test Coverage*:

* A system reset (`rst_n_i`, the bridges' AXI-side reset) asserted while a SINGLE_OP write is held on the W channel of each bridge by a responder READY stall that is released only after the reset
* The bridge observed mid-flight before the reset through `dtp_tb_if`: its TCK-domain AXI state machine on the write path, outside idle, with the single-op pending flag set (the CDC accepts AW and W at once, so the bridge waits for the write response while the responder holds the channel). The reset lands only once the port's W stall counter has advanced, so W waits on the bus: the bridge moves a request into its CDC only on TCK edges.
* Containment: the state machine back to idle within 128 TCK cycles after the reset, the CDC's TCK-side isolate-and-clear observed, and no write for the aborted operation reaching the subordinate
* Recovery without a TAP reset: the SINGLE_OP status leaving `BUSY_OR_FULL` within eight SINGLE_OP captures (each a Capture-DR of the bridge's SINGLE_OP register from Run-Test/Idle, the instruction loaded once) and reporting the discarded write as `DECERR`, and a legal write completing with OKAY afterwards
* The bench's three AXI responders reset on `rst_n_i` with the bridges, so each aborted transaction is dropped on the subordinate side of the port as well (<<dtp-vplan-scope>>)
* A bridge that stays outside idle, waiting for the write response with status `BUSY_OR_FULL` after the reset, fails `CHK-J2A-ABORT-FSM` and `CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY`
* A second leg per bridge holds a SERIES_DATA_INCR write beat on the W channel: the reset discards the series operation, SERIES_CTRL reads `DECERR` and keeps the beat's address, and after `SERIES_CTRL.reset` a beat at that address lands

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each bridge: hold the responder's W channel with a READY stall that outlasts the scenario, record the target slot's prior word, and issue a SINGLE_OP write through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`. The scenario aborts this write, so it arms no stimulus-intent record; `CHK-J2A-ABORT-ESCAPE` judges its slot, before and after the follow-on write.
. Step TCK in Run-Test/Idle while sampling the bridge FSM, the pending flag, and the port's W stall counter through `dtp_tb_if` until the FSM leaves `AXI_IDLE` and the stall counter advances, bounded in TCK cycles (the FSM and the CDC's TCK side advance only while TCK runs); record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-MIDFLIGHT`.
. Clear the sticky CDC clear-seen flag, pulse `rst_n_i` for one to three system cycles (`CHK-RESET-COUNT`), then release the stall.
. Step TCK while sampling the FSM until it returns to `AXI_IDLE`, for at most 128 TCK cycles; record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-FSM`. Record `CHK-J2A-CDC-CLEAR` from the sticky clear-seen flag and `CHK-J2A-ABORT-ESCAPE` from the unchanged target slot.
. Capture the SINGLE_OP status up to eight times, stopping when it leaves `BUSY_OR_FULL`; record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY` against `DECERR`, the status of an operation a system reset discarded, and when it matches, issue a legal write to a clean slot with the memory compare and record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-ESCAPE` on the aborted slot again.
. After all three bridges, fail the pass naming the bridges that did not report the discarded write.
. For each bridge: reset SERIES_CTRL, program a write series at a clean slot, hold the W channel, and shift one SERIES_DATA_INCR beat; step TCK until the FSM leaves `AXI_IDLE` and the W stall counter advances, and record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-MIDFLIGHT`.
a. Clear the sticky CDC clear-seen flag, pulse `rst_n_i` for one to three system cycles (`CHK-RESET-COUNT`), and release the stall; record `CHK-J2A-ABORT-FSM`, `CHK-J2A-CDC-CLEAR`, and `CHK-J2A-ABORT-ESCAPE` as for the single-op leg, then capture SERIES_CTRL and record `CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR` (the beat's address) and `CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY` against `DECERR`.
b. Reset SERIES_CTRL, program the same address, and land one beat with the memory compare; the SERIES_CTRL capture holds the next address (`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR`) and reads OKAY (`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS`).
. After all three bridges, fail the pass naming the bridges that did not report the discarded series write.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-J2A-ABORT-MIDFLIGHT` |The FSM has left `AXI_IDLE`, the pending flag is set for a single operation, and the held channel's stall counter has advanced when the reset lands
|`CHK-J2A-ABORT-FSM` |The FSM is back in `AXI_IDLE` within 128 TCK cycles after the reset
|`CHK-J2A-CDC-CLEAR` |The bridge's CDC ran its TCK-side isolate-and-clear after the reset
|`CHK-J2A-ABORT-ESCAPE` |The aborted operation's slot in the subordinate memory is unchanged, after the reset and after the follow-on write
|`CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY` |The SINGLE_OP status leaves `BUSY_OR_FULL` within eight SINGLE_OP captures and reads `DECERR` for the discarded write; the follow-on legal write then completes with OKAY and lands (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`); for the series leg, SERIES_CTRL reads `DECERR` for the discarded beat
|`CHK-J2A-SERIES-ADDR` |On the series leg, the SERIES_CTRL capture after the reset holds the discarded beat's address, and after the recovery beat the next address
|`CHK-J2A-FAULT-STATUS` |On the series leg, SERIES_CTRL reads OKAY after the recovery beat lands (`CHK-AXI-WMEM`)
|`CHK-RESET-COUNT` |The tb_top reset-assertion counter advanced by one across the pulse
|===

*Pass Criteria*: On every bridge the reset lands with the FSM observed mid-flight, the FSM returns to `AXI_IDLE`, the CDC clear is observed, no write escapes, and the status reads `DECERR` for the discarded write so the follow-on write completes, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-431-dtp_jtag2axi_cdc_clear_abort_narrow_reset_mid_xaction_test]]
===== 4.31 `dtp_jtag2axi_cdc_clear_abort_narrow_reset_mid_xaction_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Narrow system reset, then TAP reset, during a transaction

*Test Coverage*:

* A system reset (`rst_n_i`, the bridges' AXI-side reset) asserted while a SINGLE_OP write is held on the AW channel of each bridge, with W not accepted ahead of AW, by a responder READY stall that is released only after the reset
* The bridge observed mid-flight before the reset through `dtp_tb_if`: its TCK-domain AXI state machine on the write path, outside idle, with the single-op pending flag set (the CDC accepts AW and W at once, so the bridge waits for the write response while the responder holds the channel). The reset lands only once the port's AW stall counter has advanced, so AW waits on the bus: the bridge moves a request into its CDC only on TCK edges.
* Containment: the state machine back to idle within 128 TCK cycles after the reset, the CDC's TCK-side isolate-and-clear observed, and no write for the aborted operation reaching the subordinate
* Recovery without a TAP reset: the SINGLE_OP status leaving `BUSY_OR_FULL` within eight SINGLE_OP captures (each a Capture-DR of the bridge's SINGLE_OP register from Run-Test/Idle, the instruction loaded once) and reporting the discarded write as `DECERR`, and a legal write completing with OKAY afterwards
* The bench's three AXI responders reset on `rst_n_i` with the bridges, so each aborted transaction is dropped on the subordinate side of the port as well (<<dtp-vplan-scope>>)
* The single-cycle pulse exercises the `axi_cdc_clearable` reset controllers' isolate-and-clear on the TCK side; the sticky clear-seen flag in `dtp_tb_if` is the observation
* A second single-cycle reset once the first clear has completed, with the bridge idle and nothing pending: it discards nothing, so the `DECERR` of the discarded write survives it
* A bridge that stays outside idle, waiting for the write response with status `BUSY_OR_FULL` after the reset, fails `CHK-J2A-ABORT-FSM` and `CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY`; a bridge whose second reset overwrites the `DECERR` fails `CHK-J2A-ABORT-RECOVERY`
* A second leg per bridge arms `tb_top` to assert the system reset on the clock edge that completes a SINGLE_OP read's AR handshake (`dtp_tb_if.sys_rst_on_ar_arm`), so the reset lands with the read response outstanding: the bridge discards the read, SINGLE_OP reads `DECERR` with a zero data field, and a read of the same slot then returns its word. The bench holds the bridge ports' BVALID and RVALID low while `rst_n_i` is low, so the responders, which see the reset only at their next clock edge, leave no response on the port.
* A system reset landing while the TAP holds Update-DR of a SINGLE_OP, issued as raw TMS steps with TCK idle between them, on every bridge, a write then a read. The bridge latches the operation on the first TCK edge after the pulse and moves onto its address path on the second; the CDC's clear, two TCK edges behind the reset through the clear synchronizer, reaches the bridge in the one TCK cycle its state machine spends in the address state. The request never enters the crossing, no AW, W or AR reaches the port, the state machine returns to idle on the next TCK edge, and SINGLE_OP reads `DECERR` for the discarded operation, with a zero data field for a read; a recovery access of the same direction then completes.
* The TAP-reset legs: a TAP reset while a SINGLE_OP waits on the fabric against a responder READY stall, on all three bridges in both directions, a read held on AR and a write held on AW and W. The reset is TRST, asserted with one to four TCK cycles clocked under it and held until the TAP leaves Test-Logic-Reset, or five TMS-high cycles into Test-Logic-Reset, a seeded choice per leg.
